Beyond standard proxy generative tasks, we show that GeoCore-9B can be effectively adapted for practical EO applications, including highly challenging tasks such as cloud removal and SAR-to-optical cross-modal translation. Extensive evaluations confirm that GeoCore-9B establishes new state-of-the-art performance in both visual fidelity and geographic structural accuracy.

</details>


### [193] [SpatioLM: Towards General Physical Spatial Intelligence in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01899)
*Jing Wu,Jianhua Wu,Jiayi Guan,Jiahong Chen,Jinghui Lu,Hangjun Ye,Bingzhao Gao,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatioLM，一种参数高效的视觉语言模型，通过即插即用的空间视觉模块和伪深度/相机信息监督，在不引入额外3D先验或第三方空间编码器的情况下，显著提升VLM的空间推理能力，并在VSI-Bench上取得71.6分（首个超过70的模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在常识推理上表现良好，但在视觉空间推理上存在不足。已有解决方案要么引入额外3D先验输入，要么使用外部空间编码器，这增加了系统复杂度，且空间微调后会损害VLM原有的通用能力。因此需要一种既能增强空间智能又不影响通用能力的参数高效方法。

Method: 提出SpatioLM框架，包含两个关键设计：(1) 一个即插即用、非侵入式的空间视觉模块，用于激发VLM中固有的空间知识；(2) 创新性地利用伪深度（pseudo depth）和相机信息作为监督信号，引导模型学习物理上连贯的表示。该方法无需额外3D先验输入或第三方空间编码器，参数高效。

Result: 在空间感知和理解等多个任务上取得显著提升，同时有效限制通用能力的退化。在VSI-Bench上取得71.6分（首个超过70的模型），在具身操作任务迁移上也表现良好。

Conclusion: SpatioLM证明可以通过参数高效的即插即用模块和伪深度监督来增强VLM的空间智能，无需复杂的3D先验输入或外部编码器，同时保留模型的通用能力，并展示了向具身操作任务迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform well on commonsense reasoning tasks but struggle with visual spatial reasoning. Most existing solutions introduce extra 3D prior inputs or external spatial encoders, which increase complexity and degrade the underlying VLMs' general-purpose capabilities after spatial fine-tuning. To this end, we propose a parameter-efficient \textit{\textbf{Spatio}-vision \textbf{L}anguage \textbf{M}odels (SpatioLM)}, that enhances spatial intelligence without extra 3D prior inputs or third-party spatial encoders. Concretely, we design a plug-and-play and non-invasive spatio-vision module that elicits the spatial knowledge inherent in VLMs. Furthermore, we innovatively leverage pseudo depth and camera information as supervision to guide the model in learning physically coherent representations. Extensive experiments show that SpatioLM achieves significant improvements in diverse tasks, including spatial perception and understanding while effectively limiting the degradation of general capabilities. Notably, the model achieves an impressive score of 71.6 on the VSI-Bench (the first model to surpass 70). In addition, it attains competitive performance when transferred to embodied manipulation tasks. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/xiaomi-research/spatio-lm}{\faGithub~spatio-lm}.

</details>


### [194] [PhotoHOI: Synthesizing 3D Hand-Object Interactions from a Single RGB Photograph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01905)
*Zhenhao Zhang,Jiajun Zhang,Wei Min,Yebin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhotoHOI，一种从单张RGB照片和开放词汇语言指令合成3D手-物交互序列的方法。它利用视觉语言模型解析任务规格，恢复任务相关的3D场景并规划碰撞感知的物体轨迹，通过学习可迁移的接触先验和抓取先验来合成手部运动，并在学习的潜在空间中细化抓取姿态。实验在GRAB和H2O数据集上验证了优越的接触质量和穿透减少性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手-物交互（HOI）方法通常需要预定义的物体几何、物体轨迹或任务特定条件，限制了对自然真实世界输入的使用。作者希望解决更实际的问题：仅从单张RGB照片和开放词汇语言指令即可合成3D HOI序列，消除对物体先验信息的依赖。

Method: PhotoHOI包含三个关键阶段：(1) 使用视觉语言模型（VLM）将输入图像和指令解析为结构化任务规格（交互物体、目标区域、空间关系）；(2) 恢复紧凑的任务相关3D场景，基于物体状态、支撑关系和周围场景几何规划平滑的碰撞感知物体轨迹；(3) 从大规模affordance和HOI数据中学习可迁移的任务条件接触先验和接触条件抓取先验来合成手部运动，并在学习的潜在空间中细化抓取姿态，约束优化到合理的姿态流形。

Result: 在GRAB和H2O数据集上的实验表明，该方法在接触质量和穿透减少方面优于代表性基线。在真实世界照片上的结果进一步展示了更高的任务成功率和场景一致性，以及对未见物体和开放词汇指令的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PhotoHOI成功实现了从单张照片和自然语言指令直接合成3D手-物交互序列，克服了现有方法对预定义物体条件的依赖，并展现出对真实世界场景和未见物体的良好泛化能力，在AR/VR、数字人和具身交互等应用中有广阔前景。

Abstract: Hand-object interaction (HOI) is a fundamental human behavior with broad applications in AR/VR, digital humans, and embodied interaction. Existing methods typically require predefined object geometry, object trajectories, or task-specific conditions, limiting their use with natural real-world inputs. To address this, we study a more practical problem of synthesizing 3D hand-object interaction sequences from a single RGB photograph and an open-vocabulary language instruction, and introduce PhotoHOI. PhotoHOI first uses a vision-language model to parse the input image and instruction into a structured task specification, including the interaction object, target region, and spatial relation. It then recovers a compact task-relevant 3D scene and plans a smooth collision-aware object trajectory based on the recovered object states, support relations, and surrounding scene geometry. To synthesize hand motion that generalizes to real-world photographs and unseen objects, it learns transferable task-conditioned contact and contact-conditioned grasp priors from large-scale affordance and HOI data. The grasp is further refined in a learned latent space, constraining the optimization to a plausible hand-pose manifold. Experiments on GRAB and H2O demonstrate improved contact quality and reduced penetration over representative baselines. Results on real-world photographs further demonstrate higher task success and scene consistency, together with generalization to unseen objects and open-vocabulary instructions.

</details>


### [195] [Assessing the Benefits of Combining Advanced Deep Learning Techniques for Post-Disaster Building Damage Assessment from UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01906)
*Huy Quang Ung,Guillaume Habault,Roberto Legaspi,Hao Niu,Lian Cao,Masato Taya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合框架，将CV模型的精确检测能力与LVLM的强推理能力解耦结合，用于灾后建筑损毁评估。CV模型先检测建筑物并生成边界框，LVLM再据此进行损毁分类和上下文解释。在RescueNet和FloodNet基准上，最佳组合在建筑物计数上超越独立基线最高2.1个R²点，且仅需有限的标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后建筑损毁评估至关重要但具挑战性。现有CV模型需要大量标注数据、跨区域泛化能力差且局限于特定训练任务；而LVLM虽具有强推理和泛化能力，但在低层感知任务（如目标检测和精确边界框生成）上表现不足，且微调需要大量数据。因此需要结合两者优势的新框架。

Method: 提出混合框架将检测与损毁评估解耦：1) CV模型（如检测器）首先在图像上检测建筑物并生成边界框；2) 将检测结果传递给LVLM进行损毁分类（完好/部分损毁/完全损毁）和上下文语义解释。框架在RescueNet和FloodNet两个真实基准上进行评估。

Result: 最佳组合在准确计数完好、部分损毁和完全损毁建筑物方面，超越独立基线最高2.1个R²点，且仅需有限的检测阶段标注数据。此外提供了失败场景和边界情况的详细分析，为实践者提供实用见解。

Conclusion: 混合框架有效结合了CV的精确感知与LVLM的推理能力，在少量标注数据条件下实现了更好的灾后损毁评估性能，并公开了源代码和数据以供研究社区使用。

Abstract: Rapid and accurate post-disaster building damage assessment is essential, yet remains a challenging task. Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) imagery offers a timely and high-resolution view of affected areas, but existing Computer Vision (CV) models often demand large annotated datasets, generalize poorly across geographic regions and their assessment policies, and are confined to the specific tasks they were trained for. Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) offer a promising alternative through their strong reasoning and generalization capabilities, but fall short on precise, low-level perception tasks such as object detection and accurate bounding box generation. Furthermore, they often require a substantial amount of data for effective fine-tuning on domain-specific tasks. In this paper, we propose a hybrid framework that decouples detection from damage assessment, combining the precision of CV models with the reasoning power of LVLMs. A CV model first detects buildings and generates bounding boxes on the image that are then passed to an LVLM for damage classification and contextual interpretation. We evaluated our framework on two real-world benchmarks: RescueNet and FloodNet. In particular, the best combination under this framework accurately counts intact, partially damaged and completely destroyed buildings, surpassing isolated baselines by up to 2.1 R^2 points, while requiring only limited annotated data for the detection stage. Beyond reporting aggregate gains, we provide a detailed analysis of failure scenarios and edge cases, offering practical insights for practitioners and concrete directions for future work. Our source code and data are publicly available to the research community via the following repository: https://github.com/ungquanghuy-kddi/VLM_GDINO.git

</details>


### [196] [PNEC-Mamba: Prototype-Guided Positive-Negative Evidence Calibration for Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01910)
*Mingzhen Xu,Can Xu,Di Wang,Haonan Guo,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PNEC-Mamba是一个面向高光谱图像分类的原型引导正负证据校准框架，从像素级证据可靠性建模视角出发，通过动态类原型、正负证据分离、多源不确定性估计和全分辨率一致性优化，在三个基准数据集上取得了优于现有方法的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像中像素表示常因光谱相似性、混合像素和局部上下文干扰而存在歧义，现有方法主要关注学习更强的表示或建模更广泛的上下文，却很少研究学习到的表示是否提供了可靠证据或在分类决策中引入了干扰。

Method: 提出PNEC-Mamba框架，包含四个核心组件：(1) 全图像状态空间编码器提取像素表示，动态类原型提供随特征空间联合演化的语义参考；(2) 通过像素-原型竞争分离正负证据，显式区分支持分类的判别线索与竞争类别的混淆信号；(3) 基于证据关系引入多源不确定性估计策略，对不确定区域进行更强的证据校准；(4) 全分辨率一致性细化步骤恢复局部空间细节并改善边界一致性。

Result: 在三个基准高光谱数据集上的大量实验表明，PNEC-Mamba在分类性能上优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 从像素级证据可靠性角度重新审视高光谱图像分类问题，通过正负证据分离和不确定性驱动的选择性校准，有效解决了像素歧义带来的分类干扰问题，验证了证据可靠性建模在高光谱分类中的有效性。

Abstract: In real-world hyperspectral scenes, pixel representations are often ambiguous due to factors such as spectral similarity, mixed pixels, and local context interference, which may simultaneously encode discriminative evidence and interfering information. Existing methods mainly focus on learning more powerful representations or modeling broader contexts, but rarely investigate whether the learned representations provide reliable evidence or introduce interference into classification decisions. To address this issue, we view hyperspectral image classification from the perspective of pixel-level evidence reliability modeling and propose PNEC-Mamba, a prototype-guided positive-negative evidence calibration framework. The framework progressively establishes semantic references, separates class-related evidence from interference, estimates pixel-level reliability, and performs selective calibration. First, a full-image state-space encoder extracts pixel representations, while dynamic class prototypes provide semantic references that evolve jointly with the feature space. Subsequently, positive and negative evidence is derived from pixel-prototype competition, explicitly separating discriminative cues that support classification from confusing signals associated with competing classes. Based on these evidence relationships, a multi-source uncertainty estimation strategy is introduced to assess pixel-level reliability, enabling stronger evidence calibration for uncertain regions. Finally, a full-resolution consistency refinement step is applied to recover local spatial details and improve boundary coherence in the final predictions. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that PNEC-Mamba achieves superior classification performance compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [197] [CHOW-SLAM: Compact Hybrid Representation with Complementary Overlap Window Optimization for RGB-D SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01914)
*Wenxuan Ji,Jin Xiao,Xiaoguang Hu,Jiaqi Shi,Zichong Jia,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHOW-SLAM是一个基于NeRF的稠密RGB-D SLAM框架，通过构建互补的空间约束（紧凑参数哈希混合表示）和时间约束（重叠窗口策略）来同时实现高质量场景重建和精确相机跟踪，在多个数据集上超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于NeRF的SLAM系统在有限在线资源下，难以同时构建两种约束：源自场景表示的紧凑且具有判别力的空间约束，以及源自历史观测的持久时间约束。这一难点制约了系统在重建质量和跟踪精度上的综合表现。

Method: 1) 空间上提出紧凑参数哈希（P-H）混合表示，基于平面和网格按尺度组织组件，并通过统一多输出解码器对齐TSDF和密度引起的射线终止分布；2) 时间上提出互补重叠窗口策略，在固定预算内保留近期帧、选择高重叠局部帧并引入时间分布的历史关键帧，防止优化被短期重叠或弱相关历史观测主导；3) 采用损失感知的关键帧插入和束调整调度适应跟踪质量；4) 使用ORB特征跟踪和几何位姿估计初始化，再通过神经渲染优化提升跟踪稳定性。

Result: 在多个数据集上的广泛评估表明，CHOW-SLAM在场景重建质量和相机跟踪精度两方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: CHOW-SLAM通过显式构建互补的空间和时间约束，有效解决了资源受限下NeRF-SLAM系统中两类约束难以同时构建的问题，证明了混合场景表示与自适应时间窗口策略在稠密SLAM中的有效性。

Abstract: Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) based on Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) enables dense, continuous scene reconstruction. However, existing systems operating with limited online resources struggle to simultaneously construct two types of constraints, namely, compact yet discriminative spatial constraints derived from scene representations and persistent temporal constraints derived from historical observations. To address this challenge, we propose CHOW-SLAM, a dense RGB-D SLAM framework that explicitly constructs these complementary spatial and temporal constraints. Spatially, we propose a compact parametric-hash (P-H) hybrid representation that organizes components based on planes and grids across scales in P and H branches. A unified multi-output decoder further aligns the ray termination distributions induced by TSDF and density, preserving geometry and appearance under a compact parameter budget. Temporally, we propose a complementary overlap-window strategy to prevent optimization from being dominated by short-term overlap or weakly related historical observations. Within a fixed budget, the strategy retains recent frames, selects high-overlap local frames, and introduces temporally distributed historical keyframes. Loss-aware keyframe insertion and bundle adjustment scheduling further adapt optimization to tracking quality. In addition, ORB-based tracking and geometric pose estimation are used for pose initialization, followed by neural rendering optimization to improve tracking stability. Extensive evaluations on multiple datasets demonstrate that CHOW-SLAM outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both scene reconstruction quality and camera tracking accuracy. The source code is available at https://github.com/jinjidexiaohuoban/CHOW-SLAM.

</details>


### [198] [Recompute or Reuse? Diagnosing and Mitigating Textual Shortcuts in VLM Self-Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01930)
*Wenxiao Fan,Jingling Fu,Fang Li,Luohang Liu,Yu He,Lichen Ma,Zhiyang Yu,Weishan Bi,Junshi Huang,Yan Li,Gu Simiu,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型（VLM）在视觉证据变化时，会倾向于复用先前思维链（CoT）中携带证据的文本内容，而非基于当前图像重新推理。作者提出训练无关的干预方法FSAF（Fresh-State Attention Firewall），通过隔离新鲜计算与旧CoT来抑制这种陈旧文本捷径的影响，实验证明该方法能显著提高视觉更新率并降低先验答案率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM被期望在视觉证据变化时修订推理，但失败原因常被归因于视觉注意力不足或情境惯性，缺乏对模型"复用什么而非重新计算"的明确理解。作者希望揭示先前的思维链推理可能形成与视觉重计算竞争的文本捷径这一机制性根源。

Method: 1）在16个VLM上进行匹配的反事实分析，系统移除/重排先验CoT中不同类型的文本内容（证据内容、非证据上下文、最终答案段），观察其对答案偏好的影响；2）基于发现提出FSAF（Fresh-State Attention Firewall），一种训练无关的注意力层干预方法，将当前输入的新鲜计算与先验CoT的陈旧文本信息相隔离。

Result: 1）证据性内容是先验CoT影响的最强载体，移除它比移除长度匹配的非证据上下文或最终答案段更能改变答案偏好；2）重排证据内容也会削弱先验控制，说明其组织方式调节捷径强度；3）在答案修正后捷径仍有残余影响；4）FSAF在5个VLM上将视觉更新率从35.28%提升至53.61%，并将先验答案率从39.22%降至3.67%。

Conclusion: 可靠的VLM自我反思不仅需要"再看一眼"，还必须保护新鲜的视觉重计算免受陈旧文本复用的干扰。FSAF提供了一种有效的训练无关干预手段，为提升VLM在动态视觉环境中的推理可靠性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are expected to revise their reasoning when visual evidence changes. Failures to do so are often attributed to insufficient visual attention or contextual inertia, leaving unclear what models reuse instead of recomputing from the current image. We show that evidence-bearing reasoning in a prior chain of thought (CoT) can form a textual shortcut that competes behaviorally with visual recomputation. Across 16 VLMs, a matched counterfactual analysis identifies evidence-bearing content as the most robust carrier of prior-CoT influence. Removing this evidence-bearing content shifts answer preference more than removing length-matched non-evidence context or the final-answer span, with prior control weakening progressively as more stale evidence is removed. Reordering this evidence also weakens prior control, showing that its organization modulates shortcut strength. Beyond the immediate answer, the shortcut can retain residual influence after answer correction: weakening current-image support shifts preference back toward the prior answer, while repeated prior answers and reused premises arise mainly when the shortcut remains active. To limit this influence, we introduce Fresh-State Attention Firewall (FSAF), a training-free intervention that isolates fresh computation from the prior CoT. Across five VLMs, FSAF raises visual update rate from 35.28% to 53.61% and reduces prior-answer rate from 39.22% to 3.67%. Reliable VLM self-reflection therefore requires more than looking again: fresh visual recomputation must be protected from stale textual reuse.

</details>


### [199] [CultureVidBench: Benchmarking Cultural Understanding in Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01942)
*Xianjing Han,Yuhan Su,Yang Deng,Dong Ma,Wee Peng Tay,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CultureVidBench，一个用于评估文生视频（T2V）模型文化理解能力的综合基准，包含1,000个覆盖12国、6大洲、8个文化区域和14个文化维度的提示词，通过人类用户研究和MLLM自动评估对7个代表性T2V模型进行评测，发现现有模型虽在语义遵从和视觉质量上表现良好，但难以准确捕捉细粒度文化细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V生成模型发展迅速，但对其在不同文化语境中的表现缺乏系统性评估。现有基准主要关注感知质量、物理合理性和文-视频对齐，未能直接评估生成的视频是否准确捕捉文化特定的物体、行为、仪式、可见文字或音频线索，因此需要专门的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建CultureVidBench基准，包含1,000个精心设计的提示词，覆盖12个国家、6大洲、8个文化区域和14个文化方面，分为物质文化、社会实践与表演、仪式与典礼三大类。与传统基准不同，该基准特别强调动态和多模态的文化表征，包括社会互动、仪式流程以及文化适宜的可见文字和音频。通过人类研究者和MLLM自动评估两种方式，从文化忠实度、多模态文化渲染、语义遵从和感知质量四个维度对7个代表性T2V模型进行评测。

Result: 评测结果显示，当前T2V模型在语义遵从和视觉质量方面表现较强，但在忠实捕捉细粒度文化细节方面经常失败，特别是对于代表性不足的地区、仪式场景以及多模态文化线索（如文字和音频）的呈现存在明显不足。

Conclusion: CultureVidBench揭示了T2V模型在文化理解方面的显著局限，指出现有模型缺乏对多样化文化语境，尤其是欠代表性地区和多模态文化元素的细粒度表征能力，为未来T2V模型的改进方向提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation models have advanced rapidly, yet their ability to represent diverse cultural contexts remains underexplored. Existing benchmarks mainly focus on perceptual quality, physical plausibility, and text-video alignment, but do not directly assess whether generated videos capture culturally specific objects, actions, rituals, visible text, or audio cues. We introduce CultureVidBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating cultural understanding in T2V generation. CultureVidBench contains 1,000 curated prompts covering 12 countries, 6 continents, 8 cultural regions, and 14 cultural aspects organized into three categories: material culture, social practice & performance, and ritual & ceremony. Designed specifically for video generation, CultureVidBench emphasizes dynamic and multimodal cultural representation, including social interactions, ritual procedure, and culturally appropriate visible text and audio. We evaluate seven representative T2V models through human user studies and MLLM-based automatic assessment across cultural faithfulness, multimodal cultural rendering, semantic adherence, and perceptual quality. Results show that although current models achieve strong semantic adherence and visual quality, they often fail to faithfully capture fine-grained cultural details, particularly for underrepresented regions, rituals, and multimodal cultural cues.

</details>


### [200] [UniMoCa: Unifying Motion and Camera Controls as Visual Proxies for Faithful Human Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01944)
*Liming Tan,Ye Chen,Hao Zhang,Lirong Qian,Feifei Li,Bingbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniMoCa提出了一种基于Motion-Camera Visual Proxy (MCVP)的统一框架，将3D人体运动和相机轨迹转换为身份中性的视觉代理表示，在视觉空间中统一了运动和相机控制，使两者可共同推理和编辑，并在Wan2.2 I2V上验证了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体导向视频生成方法中，运动控制依赖像素对齐的视觉序列（如骨骼图、姿态图），而相机控制依赖非视觉的几何嵌入（如相机参数），这种异构控制接口使得模型难以正确归因运动与相机效果，且对相机估计误差敏感，尤其在多人、大动作、遮挡和动态相机的复杂场景下问题突出。

Method: 提出UniMoCa框架，核心是MCVP（Motion-Camera Visual Proxy）表示：将从驱动视频中提取的3D人体运动和相机轨迹转换为身份中性的视觉代理，在恢复的相机轨迹下渲染时间对齐的人体几何，并附加显式相机轨迹标记。同时构建了MCVP-Video数据集，覆盖复杂动作、多人交互和多样相机轨迹。

Result: 基于Wan2.2 I2V的实验表明，UniMoCa在人体运动控制、相机控制、时间一致性和相机感知鲁棒性方面均取得显著提升，且仅增加极小的额外复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过将运动和相机控制统一到同一视觉表示空间，异质控制变为了相互兼容的视觉线索，实现了更一致的运动-相机联合推理与编辑，为复杂多人场景下的可控视频生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Controlling human motion and camera movement is essential for faithful human-oriented video generation, yet remains challenging in multi-person scenes with large body motions, occlusions, and dynamic cameras. Existing pipelines typically rely on visual motion sequences, such as skeleton maps, pose maps, or rendered body representations, for motion control, while using camera embeddings for camera control. Such heterogeneous control interfaces force video generation models to reconcile pixel-aligned visual cues with non-visual geometric embeddings, making motion-camera attribution difficult and sensitive to camera estimation errors. We propose \textbf{UniMoCa}, a representation-driven framework that unifies motion and camera controls in visual space. At the core of UniMoCa is \textbf{Motion-Camera Visual Proxy} (\textbf{MCVP}), a mutually-sharable novel representation that converts 3D human motion and camera trajectories extracted from driving videos into an identity-neutral visual proxy. MCVP renders temporally aligned human geometry under the recovered camera trajectory and augments it with explicit camera trajectory markers, replacing heterogeneous visual-parametric controls with distinguishable visual cues. As both control factors are represented in the same visual space, they become mutually compatible rather than heterogeneous, enabling consistent joint reasoning and editing during video generation. We further curate a \textbf{MCVP-Video} dataset covering complex actions, multi-person interactions, and diverse camera trajectories. Experiments based on the Wan2.2 I2V show that UniMoCa achieves substantial gains in human motion control, camera control, temporal consistency, and camera-aware robustness with minimal additional complexity. More details are shown in our Project page: https://tanliming-daniel.github.io/UniMoCa/.

</details>


### [201] [Event ActivityNet: A Large-Scale Simulated-Event Benchmark for Untrimmed Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01948)
*Cheng-Yao Hong,Ting-Wei Lin,Yun-Chung Lai,Hua-Wei Lee,Hwann-Tzong Chen,Tyng-Luh Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Event ActivityNet，一个大规模模拟事件基准数据集，从ActivityNet视频派生而来，包含3,263个视频、200个动作类别和106.94小时数据，支持长时程事件理解任务，并通过渐进式嵌套尺度训练协议显著提升了识别和时序定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程基于事件的动作理解尚未被充分探索，原因在于现有数据集多为短裁剪片段，而收集带有密集时间标注的原生事件流成本高昂。需要一个大规模的长时程事件基准来推动该领域研究。

Method: 从人工标注的未裁剪ActivityNet视频派生事件数据；生成5-bin和9-bin事件体素表示；将事件体素直接从非插值源视频按解码帧序生成；保留帧率元数据用于近似时间映射；使用动作中心重建LPIPS作为软诊断指标；建立自适应事件分帧、提示-字幕对齐以及事件-only、RGB-only和RGB-事件定位的基线和渐进式嵌套尺度训练协议。

Result: 在渐进式嵌套尺度训练协议下，识别Top-1准确率从52.25提升到66.42，在线时序定位平均mAP从21.7提升到29.0。分阶段的Event ActivityNet预训练后接原生事件微调，在多种监督预算下始终优于仅目标训练和从零开始的联合训练。

Conclusion: Event ActivityNet为长时程事件建模提供了可扩展的基准数据集，但原生相机评估对于面向部署的结论仍然必不可少。

Abstract: Long-horizon event-based action understanding remains underexplored because existing datasets largely comprise short, trimmed clips, while collecting native event streams with dense temporal annotations is costly. We introduce Event ActivityNet, a large-scale simulated-event benchmark derived from human-annotated, untrimmed ActivityNet videos. It comprises 3,263 videos, 200 action classes, and 106.94 hours, with matched 5-bin and 9-bin event-voxel representations, temporal action annotations, and timestamped captions. The benchmark supports annotated-segment action recognition, auxiliary event-language alignment, and causal online temporal action localization. We generate event voxels directly from non-interpolated source videos in decoded frame order, retain per-video rational nominal or average frame-rate metadata for approximate time mapping, and use action-center reconstruction LPIPS as a soft diagnostic of retained reconstructable content. We establish baselines for adaptive event framing, prompt-caption alignment, and event-only, RGB-only, and RGB-event localization. Under a progressive nested-scale training protocol, recognition Top-1 accuracy increases from 52.25 to 66.42, while online temporal localization average mAP improves from 21.7 to 29.0. Moreover, staged Event ActivityNet pretraining followed by native-event fine-tuning consistently outperforms target-only and joint-from-scratch training across multiple supervision budgets. Event ActivityNet provides a scalable benchmark for long-horizon event modeling, although native-camera evaluation remains essential for deployment-oriented conclusions.

</details>


### [202] [StyleForge: Indoor Furniture Styling by Counterfactual Reasoning in a Hypergraph Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01954)
*Lingwei Dang,Shishuo Shang,Pan Liu,Jiajia Cheng,Ziyan Qiu,Zhenhao Zhang,Yufei Zhu,Shenghui Huang,Qingxin Xiao,Yun Hao,Juntong Li,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StyleForge是一个场景级结构化挑选框架，用于固定布局室内家具风格搭配，通过动态超图风格场、冻结的多模态大语言模型提取风格先验，以及反事实风格偏好学习，解决独立检索导致的外观冲突问题，在3D-FRONT上达到SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在固定布局家具挑选中通常独立检索每个家具或依赖静态局部关系，导致场景组合后出现形状、材质和颜色冲突。需要一种能捕捉家具间高阶依赖关系的场景级挑选框架。

Method: StyleForge包含三个核心组件：(1) 冻结的多模态大语言模型从开放式风格请求和固定布局中提取结构化风格先验；(2) 动态超图风格场根据目标风格自适应激活和加权布局诱导的超边，捕捉家具间高阶依赖；(3) 反事实风格偏好学习，通过马氏距离能量评估每个候选项的上下文兼容性，训练时交替优化风格场和候选logits，推理时仅通过测试时训练更新房间特定候选logits。

Result: 在3D-FRONT数据集上的实验表明，StyleForge在家具检索和场景级风格一致性方面均达到SOTA，比对象级和场景级检索基线产生更一致的固定布局家具布置。

Conclusion: StyleForge通过场景级结构化挑选和动态超图建模，有效解决了固定布局家具搭配中的跨槽位风格冲突问题，验证了利用全局场景上下文进行家具挑选的优越性。

Abstract: Fixed-layout indoor furniture styling requires selecting assets that form a coherent room without changing the prescribed furniture categories, positions, orientations, or scales. Existing approaches typically retrieve each asset independently or rely on static local relations, making them prone to shape, material, and color conflicts after scene composition. We introduce StyleForge, a scene-level structured selection framework built on a dynamic hypergraph style field. A frozen multimodal large language model extracts structured style priors from an open-ended style request and the fixed layout, while StyleForge maintains a learnable candidate distribution for each furniture slot. Conditioned on the target style, the dynamic hypergraph style field adaptively activates and weights layout-induced hyperedges to capture higher-order dependencies among furniture. Counterfactual style preference learning then treats each candidate as a local substitution in the current style field and evaluates its contextual compatibility using Mahalanobis energies. Training alternates between optimizing the style field and the candidate logits. At inference, the model remains frozen and test-time training updates only room-specific candidate logits, progressively correcting cross-slot style conflicts as the global scene context evolves. Experiments on 3D-FRONT demonstrate state-of-the-art furniture retrieval and scene-level style coherence, producing more coherent fixed-layout furniture arrangements than object- and scene-level retrieval baselines.

</details>


### [203] [FAST-GS: Frequency Aware Space-time Gaussian Splatting for Photorealistic Dynamic Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01958)
*Zhengyang Zhang,Ziyu Lu,PengCheng Li,Hongbo Duan,Yi Liu,Pengting Luo,Peiyu Zhuang,Xinghui Li,Shaohua Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于傅里叶运动建模的4D高斯泼溅方法，通过将运动分解为频域正弦分量来捕捉复杂动态场景中的高频运动细节和低频全局轨迹，同时引入运动感知正则化策略抑制高频抖动，在保持实时渲染能力的同时提升复杂运动拟合精度和长期时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4DGS方法仅使用单一多项式建模运动，在包含高频运动分量的复杂动态场景中表现受限，且因轨迹累积漂移无法保证长期稳定性。

Method: 提出傅里叶运动建模模块，将运动分解为基于频率的正弦分量，同时捕捉低频全局轨迹和高频局部细节；并集成运动感知正则化策略到损失函数中，使用频率相关权重抑制高频抖动同时保持低频运动一致性。

Result: 在N3V和Google Immersive数据集上的多个场景进行了大量实验，验证了方法的有效性，相比现有方法在复杂运动拟合和长期一致性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持4DGS实时渲染能力的同时，通过频域视角重新设计运动建模方式，有效解决了复杂动态场景下的运动拟合和长期稳定问题，为动态场景重建提供了新的思路。

Abstract: 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) excels in dynamic 3D reconstruction and real-time novel view synthesis via efficient 4D Gaussian representations and parallelizable rendering. However, existing 4DGS approaches rely on a single polynomial to model motion, which limits performance in complex dynamic scenes where high-frequency motion components are prevalent, and fails to ensure long-term stability due to cumulative trajectory drift. To address these issues, we propose a Fourier Motion Modeling module: this paradigm decomposes motion into frequency-based sinusoidal components, capturing both low-frequency global trajectories and high-frequency local details to model complex motion patterns accurately. It retains the real-time rendering capability of 4DGS while improving complex motion fitting and long-term coherence. Additionally, we integrate a motion-aware regularization strategy into the loss function: it uses frequency-dependent weights to suppress high-frequency jitter while preserving low-frequency motion coherence. Extensive experiments on N3V and Google Immersive datasets from multiple scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [204] [OSSDD - a New Open Dataset for Sentinel-1 Ship Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01963)
*Horst Hammer,Sylvia Hochstuhl,Antje Thiele,Tobias Brosch,Padraig Davidson,Tim Remiger,Michael Teutsch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的大规模SAR船舶检测数据集OSSDD，基于OpenSARShip 1.0构建，包含15,197个Sentinel-1幅度图块（VV和VH极化）、二值船舶掩膜、轴对齐和旋转边界框标注，共55,759艘船。数据集免费开放，并在Faster R-CNN、FCOS和DETR三种检测器上进行了基准实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR船舶检测在海上态势感知中至关重要（涉及非法捕鱼、走私、边境侵犯等），但现有方法需要大量训练数据，而SAR领域相比光电领域数据稀缺，已有免费数据集在可用性和易用性方面参差不齐，缺乏高质量、易用的大规模SAR船舶检测数据集。

Method: 基于公开的OpenSARShip 1.0数据集重新构建标注：生成二值船舶掩码、轴对齐边界框和旋转边界框标注，整理为15,197个Sentinel-1幅度图块（VV和VH极化），共包含55,759艘船，并将其发布到Hugging Face平台供免费使用。同时在三个主流检测模型（Faster R-CNN、FCOS、DETR）上进行实验验证。

Result: 成功构建并发布了OSSSD数据集，包含完整的掩码和两种边界框标注类型；在Faster R-CNN、FCOS、DETR三种检测器上的实验结果作为基准，供后续研究对比参考。

Conclusion: OSSSD为SAR船舶检测研究提供了一个免费、结构清晰、标注丰富的大规模数据集，并通过基准实验验证了其可用性，有助于推动SAR船舶检测领域的发展。

Abstract: Ship detection in Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) images plays an important role for maritime situational awareness, especially with respect to different illegal activities at sea such as illegal fishing, smuggling or border violations. Modern ship detection methods using neural networks usually require large training datasets, which are considerably scarcer in the SAR domain than in the electro-optical domain. While several free datasets exist for this task, their availability and usability vary. In this paper, OpenSARShip-Ship Detection Dataset (OSSDD), a new dataset based on the well-known OpenSARShip 1.0 dataset is proposed for training neural networks for SAR ship detection. OSSDD is freely available and contains 15,197 Sentinel-1 amplitude patches in VV and VH polarization, binary ship masks, axis-aligned bounding box and rotated bounding box annotations for a total of 55,759 ships. The construction of the dataset, the contents and structure of the downloadable data and experiments with three common detector models (Faster R-CNN, FCOS, DETR) are shown and discussed. The results serve as benchmarks for future experiments. The dataset is available on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/sylviaHoch/OpenSARShip-Ship-Detection-Dataset.

</details>


### [205] [LongHorizon-Harness: Advancing Long-Horizon Agents for Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01964)
*Ziyu Ma,Hailang Huang,Shun Zou,Yong Wang,Shidong Yang,Yiming Hu,Fei Wei,XiangXiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LongHorizon-Harness，将长时程任务执行重构为任务状态管理问题，通过显式维护任务状态、仅用已验证的环境事实更新状态，以及"管理-执行-审计"（MEA）循环，显著提升LLM智能体在长时程任务上的表现，在多个基准上取得大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在执行长时程任务时，将任务执行、状态维护和完成度评估全部放在不断增长的上下文中，导致状态难以追踪，且错误的自我评估会传播到后续决策中，影响任务完成质量。

Method: 提出LongHorizon-Harness框架，核心是Manage-Execute-Audit（MEA）循环：（1）Manager负责显式维护任务状态并决定下一个子任务；（2）Executor使用全新上下文执行当前子任务；（3）Auditor以只读方式验证执行后的环境状态。同时设计了轻量级AgentAdapter，支持不同模型和工具链后端而无需修改其原生智能体循环。

Result: 在多个基准上取得显著提升：Qwen 3.7-Plus在WeaveBench上从51.8%提升至80.7%，在Terminal-Bench 2.1上从69.7%提升至77.2%，在OSWorld 2.0上从2.8%提升至8.3%；Claude Opus 4.7在OSWorld 2.0子集上从20.0%提升至34.3%，证明在不同模型、工具链和交互领域上均有一致增益。

Conclusion: 通过将任务状态从上下文中剥离并显式管理，配合独立验证机制，能够有效避免错误自我评估的传播，为长时程LLM智能体任务提供了一种更可靠、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly undertake long-horizon tasks that require sustained reasoning, tool use, and revision across many interdependent steps. However, existing agent harnesses maintain task execution, task state, and completion assessment within a growing context, making the state difficult to track and allowing incorrect self-assessments to propagate into later decisions. We reformulate long-horizon execution as a task-state management problem and propose LongHorizon-Harness, which maintains the task state explicitly outside execution and updates it only with facts independently verified from the environment. Its Manage-Execute-Audit(MEA) loop uses a manager to maintain the task state and determine the next subtask, a fresh-context executor to perform it, and a read-only auditor to verify the resulting environment state before the next round. A lightweight AgentAdapter supports interchangeable model and harness backends without modifying their native agent loops. LongHorizon-Harness improves Qwen~3.7-Plus from 51.8% to 80.7% on WeaveBench, from 69.7% to 77.2% on Terminal-Bench~2.1, and from 2.8% to 8.3% on OSWorld~2.0. It also raises Claude Opus~4.7 from 20.0% to 34.3% on an OSWorld2.0 subset, demonstrating consistent gains across models, harnesses, and interaction domains.

</details>


### [206] [SVGEval: A Vision-Grounded Framework for Perceptual-Quality Benchmarking and Evaluation in Text-to-SVG Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01977)
*Yiming Wang,Ye Chen,Hanqi Chen,Bingbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SVGEval是一个基于视觉的多模态基准，用于评估SVG生成质量，通过引入视觉渲染和专家标注，揭示现有模型在几何与布局判断上的不足，并训练了一个可解释的SVG质量评分器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型用于SVG生成日益增多，但缺乏可靠的评估方法。现有评估协议要么偏向代码检查，要么借用栅格图像指标，无法反映人类感知，也忽略了SVG特有的几何和空间构图等质量特征。

Method: 1) 提出SVGEval基准，显式引入视觉渲染，让模型评估渲染结果而非仅检查SVG代码；2) 通过多轮人工标注和专家精炼获得高质量标注；3) 基于SVGEval训练可解释的SVG质量评分器，输出多维度分数和文本理由。

Result: 系统性评估显示模型在语义对齐和美学上表现较好，但在几何和布局相关判断上存在明显短板。消融实验表明显式视觉基础和推理监督对空间与几何评估至关重要。

Conclusion: SVGEval为多模态时代下SVG生成的评估和改进提供了一个可靠的测试平台和实用评分工具，强调了视觉导向评估的重要性。

Abstract: Multimodal large models are increasingly used to generate scalable vector graphics (SVG), but reliable evaluation remains underexplored. Existing protocols are often code-centric or borrow raster-image metrics after rendering SVGs, which fail to reflect human perception and overlook SVG-specific qualities such as geometry and spatial composition. We introduce SVGEval, a vision-grounded multimodal benchmark for human-aligned SVG quality assessment. SVGEval explicitly incorporates visual renderings to evaluate whether models can judge the rendered outcome rather than only inspect SVG code, and provides high-quality annotations obtained via multi-round human labeling with expert refinement. Systematic evaluations across representative multimodal models reveal a clear gap: models perform relatively well on semantic alignment and aesthetics, yet struggle on geometry- and layout-related judgments. Building on SVGEval, we train an explainable SVG quality scorer that outputs multi-aspect scores with textual rationales. Ablations show that explicit visual grounding and reasoning supervision are crucial, especially for spatial and geometric assessment. SVGEval offers a reliable testbed and practical scorer for evaluating and improving SVG generation in the era of multimodal models.

</details>


### [207] [Proxy Avatar Meets Low-Rank Caching: Real-Time One-Shot Emotion-Controllable Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01978)
*Haijie Yang,Jindi Bao,Yixuan Dong,Hongliang Zhang,Jian Bi,Hao Tang,Zhenyu Zhang,Jianjun Qian,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Proxy Avatar Meets Low-Rank Caching（PALC）框架，结合高斯表情代理化身和低秩缓存技术，实现实时（real-time）的一次性（one-shot）情感可控肖像动画生成，同时保持强情感表现力和身份保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散模型的音频驱动肖像动画虽有进展，但仍面临两大挑战：一是缺乏足够的情感感知运动先验（emotion-aware motion priors），导致情感表现力不足；二是多步去噪过程中外观计算昂贵，难以实现实时推理。现有方法无法同时兼顾实时性、一次性生成和情感可控性。

Method: 提出级联框架：（1）高斯情感代理化身（Gaussian emotion proxy avatar）作为可复用运动生成器，基于单个身份训练，从音频和情感标签生成富有表现力的驱动视频；（2）大规模一次性重定向模型（one-shot retargeting model）从代理表演中提取与身份无关的运动，适配到任意目标肖像；（3）采用零样本外观复用与低秩缓存（low-rank caching）技术，在初始去噪步骤缓存参考外观特征，后续步骤用轻量级低秩适配器建模特征变化，大幅降低推理成本。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在情感表现力、身份保持动画质量上优于现有方法，同时推理成本大幅降低，实现了实时一次性肖像动画生成。

Conclusion: 通过将"运动生成"与"外观生成"解耦，并利用低秩缓存避免重复外观计算，成功在保证情感可控性和身份保持的同时实现了实时推理，为肖像动画的实用化部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Audio-driven portrait animation has advanced rapidly with diffusion-based generative models, yet real-time one-shot generation with expressive emotion control remains challenging. Existing methods often suffer from insufficient emotion-aware motion priors and expensive appearance computation during multi-step denoising. To address these issues, we propose Proxy Avatar Meets Low-Rank Caching, a cascaded framework for real-time one-shot emotion-controllable portrait animation. Instead of directly generating the target portrait from audio, our method uses a Gaussian-based emotion proxy avatar as a reusable motion generator, which is trained once on a single identity to produce expressive driving videos from audio and emotion labels. Since the proxy avatar only provides motion rather than target appearance or geometry, a large-scale one-shot retargeting model further extracts identity-independent motion from the proxy performance and adapts it to arbitrary target portraits. To improve inference efficiency, we introduce zero-shot appearance reuse with low-rank caching, which caches reference appearance features at the initial denoising step and models subsequent feature variations using lightweight low-rank adapters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves stronger emotional expressiveness, better identity-preserving animation, and substantially reduced inference cost, enabling real-time one-shot portrait animation.

</details>


### [208] [ET-Prune: Evidence-Aware Dynamic Budgeting for Visual Token Pruning in Text-Rich MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01979)
*Zizhong Ding,Junxian Li,Kai Liu,Shaoqiu Zhang,Xiao Xiao,Linghe Kong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ET-Prune是一个无需训练的视觉token剪枝框架，将剪枝视为"证据分配"问题，通过问题条件化的证据估计和动态token预算，在OCR等文本密集型任务中以约一半的token保留量达到或超越现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉token剪枝方法使用固定的token比例，这在以OCR为中心的任务中表现不佳。因为在这些任务中，关键证据往往是少量文本、标签或字段，其相关性由问题指定；不加区分的剪枝可能会删除这些关键证据，同时保留视觉上显著但无关的区域。

Method: ET-Prune包含三个核心组件：(1) 从解码器侧的部分query-key块中派生问题条件化的证据；(2) 保护文本类空间区域；(3) 将证据不确定性和密度转化为样本特定的token预算。然后通过三个渐进式中间层事件将序列移向该预算，对分散或文本密集的证据保留更多token，对集中的证据更积极地剪枝。

Result: 在六个backbone-benchmark对比中以约一半token数领先或持平所有剪枝方法。在OCRBench-v2上，Qwen3-VL-8B和InternVL3.5-8B分别领先最强剪枝基线1.80和0.68个百分点；在MMBench v1.1上，以54.45%的平均视觉token保留率达到0.8467的循环精确匹配准确率，优于Vanilla的0.8437。

Conclusion: ET-Prune展示了在文本密集型多模态推理中，证据感知的动态预算能够提供有利的质量-成本权衡，验证了从"固定比例剪枝"转向"证据驱动动态分配"的有效性。

Abstract: Visual token pruning reduces the inference cost of multimodal large language models, but a fixed token ratio is poorly matched to text-rich inputs. In OCR-centric tasks, decisive evidence can be a small number, label, or field whose relevance is specified by the question; indiscriminate pruning can erase that evidence while retaining visually salient but irrelevant regions. We present ET-Prune, a training-free framework that casts pruning as evidence allocation. It derives question-conditioned evidence from a decoder-side partial query-key block, safeguards text-like spatial regions, and converts evidence uncertainty and density into a sample-specific token floor. Three progressive middle-layer events then move the sequence toward this budget, retaining more tokens for diffuse or text-dense evidence and pruning concentrated evidence more aggressively. At the observed point estimates from one deterministic pass per configuration, ET-Prune leads or ties among pruned methods in all six backbone-benchmark comparisons at roughly half tokens. On OCRBench-v2, it leads the strongest pruned baselines by 1.80 and 0.68 percentage points on Qwen3-VL-8B and InternVL3.5-8B, respectively, while retaining about half of the visual tokens; on MMBench v1.1, it reaches 0.8467 circular exact-matching accuracy versus 0.8437 for Vanilla at 54.45% average visual-token retention. These results show a favorable observed quality-cost trade-off for evidence-aware dynamic budgeting in text-rich multimodal inference.

</details>


### [209] [AdaThinkV: Adaptive Thinking for Token-Efficient Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01980)
*Jingqi Tian,Haoji Zhang,Lin Chen,Hongbo Jin,Haonan Xu,Tianrui Zhu,Xingming Shui,Shilin Ma,Wenjing Yang,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaThinkV是一个自适应的视频推理框架，通过强化学习让视频多模态大语言模型根据问题难度自主决定是否进行显式链式推理，而非固定使用CoT。它引入ThinkGain作为推理效用的奖励信号，并设计VRPO算法解决困难问题中奖励信号不足的问题，最终在统一视频推理基准上以更少的token实现了更高的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式推理（CoT）能提升困难视频问题的性能，但对简单问题会浪费大量解码token。现有方法依赖离线难度标注、人工置信度阈值或外部路由器来判断何时需要推理，缺乏模型自主适应推理成本的能力。

Method: 提出AdaThinkV自适应框架：(1) 在强化学习阶段，对每个提示采样显式推理和直接回答两种模式的配对rollout；(2) 引入ThinkGain评估显式推理的提示级效用，平衡准确率收益与响应长度开销；(3) 提出Variance Recovery Policy Optimization (VRPO)，针对困难但可解问题中因有限探索导致所有rollout失败、奖励信号不足的情况，保留并逐步扩展这些组以恢复信息性信号；(4) 推理时在单一自回归序列中同时完成模式选择与响应生成。

Result: 在统一的视频推理评测套件上，AdaThinkV达到40.79的平均准确率，平均仅输出257.20个token，相比最强的自适应基线提升2.98个准确率点，同时减少22.7%的token消耗。

Conclusion: AdaThinkV证明了视频多模态大语言模型能够在不依赖外部标注或路由器的情况下，自主地根据问题难度自适应调整推理努力，在保持甚至提升准确率的同时显著降低推理成本，为视频理解中的高效推理提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning can improve performance on difficult video questions but often wastes decoding tokens on simple ones. We study whether a video multimodal large language model can adapt its reasoning effort to each question. We propose AdaThinkV, an adaptive framework for video reasoning that learns whether to reason explicitly without offline difficulty labels, manually tuned confidence thresholds, or an external router. During reinforcement learning, AdaThinkV samples matched rollouts in explicit reasoning and direct answering modes for each prompt. ThinkGain estimates the prompt-level utility of explicit reasoning by balancing its accuracy gain against additional response length, providing supervision for both conditional response generation and autonomous mode selection. For difficult prompts, limited rollout exploration can yield groups in which every response is unsuccessful and accuracy rewards show little variation, providing insufficient signal for learning. We therefore introduce Variance Recovery Policy Optimization (VRPO), which retains and progressively expands these groups to recover informative signals from prompts that are difficult yet solvable. At inference, AdaThinkV selects a response mode and generates the response in a single autoregressive sequence. Across a unified suite of video reasoning evaluations, AdaThinkV achieves a mean accuracy of 40.79 with an average of 257.20 output tokens, outperforming the strongest evaluated adaptive baseline by 2.98 points while using 22.7% fewer tokens. Project page: https://trilarflagz.github.io/AdaThinkV/

</details>


### [210] [DiffPrune: differentiable information throttling for token pruning in vision-language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01985)
*Landi He,Mingde Yao,Shawn Young,Lijian Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiffPrune是一种视觉语言模型（VLM）中的视觉token剪枝方法，通过信息节流器（Information Throttler）在训练时向视觉token注入方差保持噪声，使token分数直接反映信息损失而非依赖Gumbel-Softmax的松弛代理路径。在十个VLM基准上保持96.5%的完整模型准确率，同时将LLM预填充加速2.85倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM视觉token剪枝方法通常依赖Gumbel-Softmax近似离散选择，导致token分数依赖于松弛剪枝算子的行为，而非直接反映信息丢失的后果，这种不稳定代理路径使得分数学习不够可靠。

Method: DiffPrune在训练时保留所有token，通过Information Throttler按分数向每个token注入方差保持噪声来削弱其信息量：高分数token保持接近原始表示，低分数token携带更少的原始信息。损失通过这条真实的信息节流路径反向传播，从而避免松弛token选择的替代路径。推理时移除节流器，使用学习到的分数进行硬top-K剪枝。

Result: 在十个VLM基准上，DiffPrune保留了完整模型96.5%的准确率，同时将LLM预填充加速2.85倍，推理开销仅为0.69毫秒。

Conclusion: DiffPrune通过赋予token分数直接的信息含义，提供了一种更稳定、更有效的视觉token剪枝训练方法，在保持高精度的同时显著提升推理效率。

Abstract: Visual token pruning reduces the computational cost of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) by removing redundant visual tokens. The key is to learn a score that measures whether a token is useful. Existing methods typically rely on Gumbel-Softmax to approximate discrete selection during training. Such selectors make the score depend on the behavior of a relaxed pruning operator, not directly on the consequence of information loss. In this paper, we propose DiffPrune, which gives token scores a direct meaning. During training, DiffPrune keeps all tokens and weakens each token's information according to its score. If weakening a token hurts the task, the scorer is pushed to protect it; if not, the token can receive a lower score. Because the loss is differentiated through this actual information-throttling path, the scorer avoids the unstable surrogate path of relaxed token selection. DiffPrune implements this idea with an Information Throttler, which injects variance-preserving noise into visual tokens, where high-score tokens remain close to their original representations, while low-score tokens carry less original information. At inference, the throttler is removed, and hard top-K pruning is applied using the learned scores. Across ten VLM benchmarks, DiffPrune retains 96.5% of full-model accuracy while accelerating LLM prefill by 2.85x, with only 0.69 ms inference overhead. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [211] [Grounding and Explaining Visual Evidence for AI-Generated Image Detection in Human-Centric Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01988)
*Kun Guo,Yuzhou Yang,Haoyue Wang,Qichao Ying,Sheng Li,Zhenxing Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HAVE数据集（含40K真实与39K AI生成的人脸/人物图像，标注106K局部证据实例）及PAVE框架（联合真实性检测、视觉证据定位与区域对齐解释生成），通过judge-guided对齐奖励和感知感知正则化提升AI生成图像检测的可解释性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测方法缺乏可解释性，解释与定位证据区域之间存在不一致；同时现有基准对生成图像中常见的人为中心场景覆盖不足。

Method: 1) 构建HAVE数据集：40K真实+39K AI生成图像，来自10个近期生成器，含106K局部证据实例，覆盖8种证据类别，每例标注边界框和区域对齐解释。2) 提出PAVE框架：联合进行真实性预测、视觉证据定位和区域对齐解释生成；采用judge-guided对齐奖励评估区域-解释一致性与证据有效性；引入感知感知正则化，对比原始与随机掩码图像的token级预测，促进模型依赖视觉输入。

Result: 在HAVE及外部数据集上，PAVE在真实性检测、视觉证据定位和解释质量方面均表现优异。

Conclusion: 通过HAVE数据集和PAVE框架，实现了可解释、可靠且泛化性强的AI生成图像检测，为以人为中心场景的检测提供了更全面的基准与方法。

Abstract: Rapid advances in image generation models call for interpretable AI-generated image detection methods that not only determine authenticity but also provide supporting visual evidence. Existing approaches may produce inconsistencies between generated explanations and localized evidence regions, undermining the reliability of explanations for authenticity decisions. Meanwhile, existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of the diverse human-centric scenes prevalent in generated imagery. To address these limitations, we investigate authenticity detection with grounded and explainable visual evidence in human-centric scenes. We present HAVE (Human-centric AI-generated Visual Evidence), a diverse human-centric dataset comprising 40K real and 39K AI-generated images from 10 recent generators, with 106K localized evidence instances across 8 evidence categories, each annotated with a bounding box and a region-aligned explanation. We further propose PAVE, a Perception-Aware Visual Evidence framework that jointly performs authenticity prediction, visual evidence grounding, and region-aligned explanation generation. PAVE employs a judge-guided alignment reward to assess region--explanation consistency and evidence validity, together with perception-aware regularization that contrasts token-level predictions between original and randomly masked images to promote reliance on visual input. Experiments on HAVE and external datasets demonstrate strong performance in authenticity detection, visual evidence grounding, and explanation quality. Code and data will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [212] [SPARE: Structural Parameter-Free Affinity Regularization for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01990)
*Zong-Wei Hong,Jinglun Li,Shen Zhang,Yuhan Liu,Linze Li,Yao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARE，一种参数免费的扩散Transformer正则化方法，通过匹配中间token与干净潜在表示token之间的成对亲和力来加速训练收敛，无需外部编码器或投影头，在ImageNet 256×256上达到FID 1.90的最佳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer生成质量高但训练收敛慢。现有的表示正则化方法分为两类：基于目标的方法需要外部编码器和可学习投影头来对齐特征，代价昂贵；无目标的方法没有任何参考，只能排斥自身特征，丢弃了数据中的结构信息。先前工作表明空间结构而非全局语义是对齐收益的关键，因此希望利用数据中天然存在的空间结构作为训练目标。

Method: 核心洞察是干净数据潜在表示中token之间的相似度（成对亲和力）天然携带空间结构，且这种标量相似度可以直接跨特征空间比较而无需投影头。SPARE正则化器匹配中间层tokens与干净latents之间的成对亲和力，并扩展到跨图像token对（这是无目标方法默认排斥的），用单一学习目标校准两种关系类型。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256上与SiT骨干网在400K迭代预算下测试：SPARE不增加编码器、头或参数，仅增加0.08 GB训练内存，但在所有测试设置中取得参数自由正则化器中最低的FID，恢复了REPA FID降低的37-54%，与REPA结合时进一步改善，在1M迭代的classifier-free guidance下达到FID 1.90。

Conclusion: 空间结构可以直接作为训练目标，且不仅存在于单张图像内部，还存在于跨图像之间。SPARE证明了无需外部编码器或投影头，仅通过匹配跨图像的token亲和力就能有效加速扩散Transformer的训练，为参数自由的表示正则化提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Denoising diffusion transformers achieve strong generation quality but converge slowly during training. Regularizing their internal representations has emerged as an effective accelerator, yet existing methods split into two families with complementary costs. Target-based methods strengthen representations by aligning them to external features, which requires an external encoder and a learnable projection head to bridge feature spaces. Target-free methods hold no reference at all, and can only repel the model's own features across samples or layers, discarding whatever structure the data contains. Prior work suggests that spatial structure, rather than global semantics, drives the gains of alignment. We therefore ask whether such structure can serve as a target directly, and whether it exists not only within an image but across images. Our key insight is that the clean data latent already carries this structure in the relations among its tokens, where a relation is the similarity between two tokens, a single scalar comparable across feature spaces without a projection head. We propose Structural Parameter-free Affinity Regularization (SPARE), a regularizer that matches the pairwise affinities of intermediate tokens to those of the clean latents. To exploit this structure fully, SPARE extends the matching to token pairs across images, precisely the pairs that prior target-free methods repel by default, and calibrates both relation types with a single learning objective. On ImageNet $256 \times 256$ with SiT backbones under matched 400K-iteration budgets, SPARE adds no encoder, head, or parameters and only 0.08 GB of training memory, yet attains the lowest FID among parameter-free regularizers in every tested setting, recovers 37 to 54\% of REPA's FID reduction, and improves over REPA when combined with it, reaching FID 1.90 under classifier-free guidance at 1M iterations.

</details>


### [213] [ASTRA: Asynchronous Spatio-Temporal Reconstruction via Trajectory Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02006)
*Junyu Zhu,Hao Zhu,Xinzhuo Zhang,Hongdong Li,Zhan Ma,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ASTRA框架，利用2D运动轨迹作为显式、纹理无关的监督信号来解决异步多相机动态3D重建中的时间偏移估计问题，显著提升了大偏移和复杂运动场景下的重建质量与同步精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步动态3D重建方法依赖光度监督估计时间偏移，但在大偏移和复杂运动下，低纹理区域缺乏对齐信号（纹理诱导崩溃），且时间误差会被几何或运动畸变吸收（变形诱导耦合），导致重建质量严重下降。

Method: ASTRA框架通过将重建3D点的投影运动与观测到的2D轨迹对齐，联合优化时间偏移和动态3D表示；同时引入动态掩码和确定性掩码来抑制不可靠的轨迹约束。该方法不依赖渲染的颜色残差，因此不受纹理信息限制。

Result: 在多种动态Gaussian Splatting骨干网络上，ASTRA在高频空间细节保留和鲁棒性方面表现优异，即使面对高达25帧偏移的严重异步也保持稳定：PSNR提升约1.4dB，时间偏移MAE降低54.0%，同步成功率几乎提升4倍。

Conclusion: ASTRA证明了2D运动轨迹作为显式约束在异步动态重建中的有效性，克服了光度监督在纹理缺乏和大偏移场景下的固有局限，为异步多相机动态场景重建提供了更可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Dynamic 3D scene reconstruction has achieved remarkable success under the assumption of strictly synchronized multi-camera inputs. However, in real-world scenarios, temporal asynchrony among capturing devices remains a critical challenge, leading to severe motion blur and geometric artifacts. Existing asynchronous reconstruction methods typically estimate temporal offsets through photometric supervision, but appearance matching provides weak temporal cues under large offsets and complex motions. We attribute this limitation to two major bottlenecks: texture-induced collapse, where low-texture regions provide nearly vanishing alignment signals, and deformation-induced coupling, where temporal errors are absorbed into distorted geometry or motion rather than being explicitly corrected. To address these issues, we propose ASTRA (Asynchronous Spatio-Temporal Reconstruction via Trajectory Alignment), a framework that introduces 2D motion trajectories as explicit, texture-agnostic supervision for asynchronous dynamic reconstruction. Instead of synchronizing cameras solely through rendered color residuals, ASTRA jointly optimizes temporal offsets and dynamic 3D representations by aligning the projected motion of reconstructed 3D points with observed 2D trajectories, while using dynamic and certainty masking to suppress unreliable trajectory constraints. Extensive experiments on different dynamic Gaussian Splatting backbones show that ASTRA preserves high-frequency spatial details and sustains strong robustness even under severe asynchrony with up to 25-frame offsets, achieving approximately 1.4 dB PSNR improvement, reducing temporal-offset MAE by 54.0\%, and nearly quadrupling the synchronization success rate.

</details>


### [214] [Beyond Global Latents: Chunk-Based Sparse Grid VAE for Scalable 3D Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02016)
*Kaiyi Zhang,Zhihao Liang,Haolin Liu,Qingxiang Lin,Zeqiang Lai,Yunfei Zhao,Bowen Zhang,Xianghui Yang,Zibo Zhao,Chunchao Guo,Long Quan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChunkVAE是一种基于稀疏体素网格的变分自编码器，通过将全局潜在体分解为局部chunk（块）来处理高分辨率3D重建。它引入了两个关键数据算子：平衡二元对象分区（BBOP）用于分配活跃单元并限制重叠复制，以及S-Curve加权拼接用于减弱拼接边界处不可靠的特征。实验表明该方法在512³到1536³分辨率下与强基线竞争或更优，且更小的chunk降低峰值内存、缩短计算时间，实现更快的并行推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏体素网格虽然保留了3D重建所需的空间结构，但其内存随分辨率增长而迅速增加（活跃表面单元增加）。现有方法依赖全局潜在体，难以扩展至高分辨率，因此需要一种能够局部化压缩并保持全局接口的方法。

Method: 1) 提出局部chunk稀疏网格VAE架构，编码器和解码器可独立选择分区，推理chunk大小可不同于训练；2) 设计平衡二元对象分区算子，在分配活跃单元的同时限制chunk间的复制重叠；3) 设计S-Curve加权拼接算子，通过加权衰减拼接边界处的不可靠特征来组装全局潜在或重建结果。

Result: 在三个对象基准上，ChunkVAE在512³到1536³分辨率下与强基线相当或更优；更小chunk降低了峰值分配内存并缩短每chunk计算时间，实现更快的并行推理；拼接的潜在表示稳定，图像到3D的指标得到改善。

Conclusion: 局部压缩（chunk-based）可以在保持全局接口的前提下实现几何规模的扩展，证明了无需全局潜在体的稀疏体素VAE在高分辨率3D重建中的可行性和优势。

Abstract: Sparse voxel grids preserve the spatial structure needed for detailed 3D reconstruction, but their memory still grows rapidly with resolution as active surface cells increase. We introduce ChunkVAE, a sparse grid variational autoencoder organized around local chunks rather than a global latent volume. Local learned operators permit independently chosen encoder and decoder partitions and allow inference chunk sizes to differ from training. Two complementary data operators make this flexibility practical: Balanced Binary Object Partitioning distributes active cells while limiting replicated overlap, while S-Curve weighted stitching attenuates unreliable boundary features when assembling a global latent or reconstruction. Across three object benchmarks, ChunkVAE is competitive with or better than strong baselines from $512^3$ to $1536^3$; smaller chunks lower peak allocated memory and shorten per-chunk compute, enabling faster parallel inference. Stable stitched latents and improved image to 3D metrics indicate that local compression can scale geometry while retaining the global interface required downstream.

</details>


### [215] [Invisible Ink Threats: Adversarial Goals Behind Legitimate Tasks in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02018)
*Jia-Chen Zhang,Ze-Yu Zhang,Kai-Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了计算机使用智能体（CUAs）面对间接提示注入攻击的盲点，提出了"隐形墨水威胁"（Invisible Ink Threats）概念——低危害的注入目标因与合法任务执行行为无法区分，能够规避模型安全机制和人类监督。作者构建了II-Bench基准（444个对抗性示例）和HITLCUA测试框架（集成真实虚拟机与Docker隔离平台，并模拟人类参与），实验表明主流CUAs频繁被低危害注入绕过防御与模拟用户审核，揭示了当前严重且未被充分探索的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机协同（human-in-the-loop）防御范式仅能有效抵御明显的高危害攻击，但对低危害注入目标几乎无防护能力。作者指出现有研究忽视了这类"行为上与合法任务无法区分"的攻击，因此需要系统性地研究这一安全盲区。

Method: 1) 提出II-Bench基准，包含444个对抗性示例，覆盖机密性和完整性攻击，跨三个平台、三种攻击类别（页面导航与交互、敏感信息外泄、代码下载与执行），每种类别以自然语言和代码两种形式、两种指令具体程度实例化；2) 构建HITLCUA测试框架，集成真实虚拟机操作系统环境与基于Docker的隔离Web平台，通过API模拟用户参与，允许CUAs在执行可疑操作前咨询模拟用户。

Result: 对主流CUAs的广泛评估显示，低危害注入频繁地同时绕过智能体自身防御和模拟用户审核，充分暴露了当前CUAs中严重且此前未被充分探索的安全风险。

Conclusion: 低危害注入攻击（Invisible Ink Threats）是当前CUAs安全防御中一个关键盲点，现有人机协同范式不足以应对此类威胁，亟需新的防御机制来识别并防范行为上与合法任务难以区分的攻击。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs), which empower large language models to autonomously operate operating systems and the web, are increasingly vulnerable to indirect prompt injection attacks. A widely adopted defense is the human-in-the-loop paradigm, in which the agent pauses for explicit user confirmation before executing sensitive operations. While effective against conspicuously high-harm attacks, this defense offers little protection against what we term Invisible Ink Threats: low-harm injected goals, such as starring a repository or installing a package, that are behaviorally indistinguishable from legitimate task execution and thus evade both model safety mechanisms and human oversight. To systematically investigate this blind spot, we present II-Bench, a collection of seemingly harmless adversarial tasks. II-Bench comprises 444 examples targeting confidentiality and integrity attacks across three platforms, spanning three attack categories: page navigation and interaction, sensitive information exfiltration, and code download and execution. Each category is instantiated in both natural language and code forms under two levels of instruction specificity. Furthermore, we construct HITLCUA, a comprehensive adversarial testing framework that integrates a real virtual machine operating system environment with isolated Docker-based web platforms, and simulates human participation by allowing CUAs to consult an API-simulated user before proceeding with suspicious operations. Extensive evaluations of leading CUAs reveal that low-harm injections frequently bypass both agent defenses and simulated user review, exposing severe and previously underexplored security risks in current CUAs.

</details>


### [216] [RSVideo: Are Your Vision-Language Models Ready for Remote Sensing Videos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02039)
*Hongjie Zhou,Shiqin Wang,Haoyang Chen,Haonan Guo,Di Wang,Juhua Liu,Fu Lin,Yong Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RSVideo-10K，一个包含10,773个实例、147万帧、17.02小时航拍和卫星视频的遥感视频数据集，并配套RSVideo-Bench基准（2,731个测试实例）评估视觉语言模型的L1感知和L2推理能力。评估发现现有模型在小目标证据恢复、短时状态跟踪和场景约束空间推理方面存在不足，为此进一步提出RSVideo强化学习框架，通过跨帧选择问题相关区域并抑制冗余背景token实现小目标时空聚焦，在InternVL3.5-14B上取得9.01%的最大绝对提升，在Qwen3.6-27B上达到40.63%的最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感视频理解缺乏统一的评估设置，已有模型主要针对单张图像或长时间跨度的离散时序观测，无法有效评估视觉语言模型在连续遥感视频理解上的能力。需要一个统一的数据集和基准来系统评估模型在感知和推理层面的表现。

Method: 1) 构建RSVideo-10K数据集（无人机和卫星平台，17.02小时视频），包含L1感知和L2推理两大互补维度、7个能力组和17个任务；2) 基于系统评估分析，提出RSVideo强化学习框架，通过跨帧选择问题相关区域并抑制冗余背景token，实现小目标时空聚焦。

Result: 在26个开源视觉语言模型上评估，现有模型在小局部证据恢复、短时状态跟踪和场景约束空间关系使用上表现困难。RSVideo框架在InternVL3.5-14B上实现9.01%的最大绝对提升，在Qwen3.6-27B上达到40.63%的最高准确率。

Conclusion: 遥感视频理解仍是当前视觉语言模型的重大挑战，尤其在细小目标和动态时序推理方面。RSVideo-10K和RSVideo-Bench提供了统一的评估平台，而RSVideo强化学习框架展示了通过时空聚焦显著提升模型性能的可行性。

Abstract: Remote-sensing videos enable real-time observation of changes in target attributes, short-term activities, and scene evolution. They record motion, actions, interactions, and scene changes that cannot be captured by isolated images. Existing models primarily target single images or discrete temporal observations spanning a long time range. However, a unified evaluation setting for assessing vision-language models on continuous remote-sensing video understanding remains lacking. We introduce RSVideo-10K, a remote-sensing video dataset comprising 10,773 instances, 1.47 million frames, and 17.02 hours of footage, containing both unmanned aerial vehicles and satellite platforms. Its fixed evaluation benchmark, RSVideo-Bench, contains 2,731 test instances and evaluates two complementary aspects of remote-sensing video understanding: L1 Perception and L2 Reasoning, spanning seven capability groups and 17 tasks. Evaluations show that current vision-language models still struggle to recover small local evidence, track short-lived states, and use scene-constrained spatial relations. Based on this analysis, we further propose RSVideo, a reinforcement learning framework for small-target spatiotemporal focusing that selects question-relevant regions across frames and suppresses redundant background tokens. RSVideo achieves a maximum absolute improvement of 9.01% with InternVL3.5-14B and attains the highest accuracy of 40.63% with Qwen3.6-27B across 26 open-source vision-language backbones.Codes will be available at https://github.com/HongjieZhou0329/RSVideo.

</details>


### [217] [Déjà Cue: Localizing States in Object Histories via Vocabulary-Relative Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02044)
*Haofan Cao,Zhichao You,Yunkai Yang,Liang Guo,Jie Wang,Chongshou Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Déjà Cue，一种无需训练的状态时刻检索框架。通过将候选状态描述转化为词汇相对坐标系统（减去状态平衡质心），对冻结编码器的帧分数进行校准，在78个VOST视频历史中，将tIoU 0.5下的R@1从10.3%提升至20.5%，Top-1 tIoU从16.0%提升至21.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跟踪技术能关联同一物体在不同帧中的外观，但无法判断物体处于何种状态（空/满、完整/切割）。直接使用绝对图像-文本相似度来独立评估每个状态描述存在缺陷：因为所有可见帧都描绘同一目标，共享的对象兼容性会掩盖区分状态所需的证据，导致难以定位目标状态对应的视频区间。

Method: Déjà Cue是一个无需训练的框架：1）将候选状态描述作为词汇相对坐标系，从每个描述中减去状态平衡质心（state-balanced centroid）；2）利用校正后的分数对帧进行校准；3）使用冻结编码器在连续可见区间内扫描多个时长，以定位每个状态成立的时间间隔。

Result: 在78个VOST视频历史上，保持时间扫描固定、仅改变查询参考，R@1（tIoU 0.5）从10.3%提升到20.5%（几乎翻倍），Top-1 tIoU从16.0%提升至21.5%。候选排名分析表明，词汇相对查询能在相同候选集内将有效区间排名更高。

Conclusion: 相关的状态描述可以作为物体专属的、查询时刻的坐标系统来解读冻结的视觉表征。这证明了替代状态描述提供了关键的参考信息，用于衡量某一状态相对于其他状态的证据强度，而不是孤立地评估每个状态。

Abstract: Tracking links observations of the same object through visual change, yet cannot by itself determine when the object is empty or filled, intact or cut. We formulate identity-conditioned state-moment retrieval: given a tracked-object history and alternative state descriptions, localize an interval in which each described state holds. Absolute image-text similarity scores descriptions independently; because every visible frame depicts the same target, shared object compatibility can obscure the state evidence needed to identify the target interval. The alternatives provide the missing reference: evidence for one state should be measured against the others. We introduce Déjà Cue, a training-free framework that turns these alternatives into a vocabulary-relative coordinate system. It subtracts their state-balanced centroid from each description, calibrates frame scores, and scans multiple durations within contiguous visible runs using a frozen encoder. On 78 VOST histories, holding the temporal scan fixed and changing only the query reference nearly doubles R@1 at tIoU 0.5 from 10.3\% to 20.5\% and raises Top-1 tIoU from 16.0\% to 21.5\%. Candidate-rank analyses show that vocabulary-relative queries rank useful intervals higher within the same candidate set. Related state descriptions can therefore serve as an object-specific, query-time coordinate system for reading frozen visual representations.

</details>


### [218] [TBSG-Net: Temporal Bipartite Scene Graph Network for Fine-Grained Video Moment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02056)
*Ji Huang,Yongsheng Dai,Tianyu Ren,Barry Devereux,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TBSG-Net，这是首个基于动态场景图(DSG)的无提议视频时刻检索(VMR)模型，通过时间二分场景图(TBSG)显式编码对象的时空关系与时间跨度，解决静态场景图缺乏时间动态性和时间跨度编码的局限性，并在实验中大幅超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无提议视频时刻检索方法广泛采用静态场景图(SSG)来增强视频表示，但SSG存在两大固有缺陷：(1)无法建模对象及其关系随时间演化的动态过程，丢失关键的时间依赖信息；(2)未显式编码关系的持续时间，导致精确定位困难。这两个限制阻碍了SSG在VMR中的进一步应用。

Method: TBSG-Net方法包含两个核心部分：(1)利用动态场景图(DSG)提取以事件为中心的图表示，建模对象随时间交互以解决时间动态性问题；(2)设计新颖的动态场景图嵌入(DSG-E)模块，其中TBSG Constructor将DSG转换为TBSG，显式编码对象、关系和时间跨度；随后通过混合TBSG编码器（结合Transformer变体用于全局事件建模，图卷积网络用于细粒度关系推理）生成更全面的时空表示。

Result: 实验结果表明TBSG-Net在所有基线方法上均取得了显著的性能提升，验证了动态场景图在无提议视频时刻检索任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: TBSG-Net通过将动态场景图引入视频时刻检索，成功解决了静态场景图的两大局限性，为VMR任务提供了更全面的时空表示，开辟了基于动态场景图的proposal-free VMR研究方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in proposal-free Video Moment Retrieval (VMR) have highlighted the effectiveness of Static Scene Graphs (SSGs). By modeling objects and their relations at the frame level, SSGs enrich retrieval-oriented video representations. However, integrating SSGs into VMR remains constrained by two inherent limitations: (1) Lack of Temporal Dynamics. SSGs fail to model how objects and their relationships evolve over time, leading to the loss of essential temporal dependencies in video representation; and (2) Lack of Explicit Temporal Span Encoding. SSGs do not explicitly encode the duration of relationships, making precise localization challenging. To address these limitations, we propose Temporal Bipartite Scene Graph Network (TBSG-Net)---to the best of our knowledge, the first Dynamic Scene Graph (DSG) based proposal-free VMR model. Specifically, TBSG-Net leverages DSGs to extract event-centric graph representations of the input video, enabling the modeling of object interactions over time and thus addressing limitation (1). These DSGs are then processed by a novel Dynamic Scene Graph Embedding (DSG-E) module to capture both Temporal Span and spatio-temporal information. First, DSG-E utilizes a TBSG Constructor to transform DSGs into TBSGs, explicitly encoding objects, relationships, and time spans to tackle limitation (2). Second, the resultant TBSGs are passed into a hybrid TBSG Encoder that integrates a Transformer variant for global event modeling and a Graph Convolutional Network for detailed relational reasoning, ultimately producing a more comprehensive spatio-temporal representation. Our experiments demonstrate substantial improvements of TBSG-Net over all baselines.

</details>


### [219] [MIEScore: Human-Aligned Evaluation for Multi-Source Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02059)
*Zitong Xu,Huiyu Duan,Xinyun Zhang,Weifei Xiong,Tianyi Zheng,Xiongkuo Min,Qiang Hu,Zhengxue Cheng,Bo Li,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MIE-Bench，首个大规模多源图像编辑基准，包含3000个编辑实例、16个任务、36K张编辑图像及108K个人类评分；并基于此提出MIEScore多模态评估模型，通过技能优化和多维监督微调实现与人类偏好高度对齐的图像编辑质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（如Nano-Banana-Pro和GPT-Image-2）展现出多源图像编辑（MIE）能力，但现有基准和图像编辑评估方法主要聚焦单图编辑任务，忽视了更具挑战性的多源图像编辑场景，亟需一个全面且与人类对齐的MIE基准。

Method: 1) 构建MIE-Bench数据集：包含3000个编辑实例、16个任务，每个实例涉及多张源图像和编辑提示；由12个SOTA编辑模型生成36K张编辑图像，收集超108K个平均意见分数（MOS），覆盖视觉质量、指令遵循和属性保持三个维度。2) 提出MIEScore评估模型：基于多模态大语言模型，采用技能优化和多维监督微调来增强与人类偏好的对齐能力。

Result: MIEScore在人类偏好对齐方面达到最先进性能，并在其他IEQA数据集上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MIE-Bench和MIEScore填补了多源图像编辑基准和评估方法的空白，提供了大规模带细粒度人类偏好标注的数据集及高对齐度的自动评估模型，数据和模型已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in unified multimodal models have significantly improved text-guided image editing abilities. In particular, models such as Nano-Banana-Pro and GPT-Image-2 demonstrate emerging capabilities in multi-source image editing (MIE), including tasks such as object synthesis, person-background composition, and cross-image style fusion. However, existing benchmarks and image editing assessment (IEQA) methods remain primarily focused on single-image editing tasks and largely overlook the more challenging setting of MIE. This highlights the urgent need for a comprehensive and human-aligned benchmark for MIE. To this end, we introduce MIE-Bench, the first large-scale multiple image editing benchmark with fine-grained human preference annotations. Specifically, MIE-Bench includes 3,000 editing instances across 16 tasks, each involving more than two source images and an editing prompt, together with 36K edited images produced by 12 state-of-the-art editing models and over 108K mean opinion scores (MOSs) covering visual quality, instruction following, and attribute preservation. Based on MIE-Bench, we propose MIEScore, a multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based evaluation model enhanced with skill optimization and multi-dimensional supervised fine-tuning, to provide human-aligned feedback for MIE. Extensive experiments show that MIEScore achieves state-of-the-art performance in aligning with human preferences and generalizes well across other IEQA datasets. Both the dataset and the model are available at https://github.com/IntMeGroup/MIEScore.

</details>


### [220] [GIFT: Geometry-Invariant Fine-Tuning for Non-Lambertian Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02068)
*Xianghui Fan,Zhaoyu Chen,Bingqian Wu,Dayu Li,Xin Zeng,Huanran Cui,Guangzhen Xu,Xiangru Huang,Hang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GIFT是一个无需深度标签的参数高效后训练框架，利用非朗伯表面上外观变化但几何不变的特性来抑制单目深度基础模型在镜面和透明物体上的深度幻觉，同时保持模型在普通区域的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度基础模型在非朗伯表面（如镜面、玻璃）上会产生深度幻觉，错误地估计反射或透射内容而非物理表面本身。用真实世界数据适配这些模型非常困难，因为传统深度传感器在非朗伯区域同样不可靠，缺乏可用的真实深度标签来监督。

Method: GIFT利用几何不变性原理：非朗伯表面的外观随反射/透射环境变化而变化，但其底层几何保持不变。具体做法是：(1) 在保持相机和目标几何固定的条件下，收集受控外观变化下的RGB图像组；(2) 利用这些观察之间的几何不变性约束来抑制深度幻觉，采用参数高效的后训练策略，无需测量深度标签。

Result: 在自建基准和一个独立真实世界数据集上的实验表明，GIFT显著改善了镜面和透明物体的深度预测质量，同时很大程度上保留了基础模型的性能。作者还构建了一个受控基准来评估非朗伯深度恢复、对外观变化的鲁棒性以及在其他区域的性能保持情况。

Conclusion: GIFT提供了一种实用且低成本的方案，将单目深度基础模型适配到非朗伯场景，解决了深度传感器在此类区域不可靠的核心难题，无需额外深度标注即可有效抑制深度幻觉。

Abstract: Monocular depth foundation models, benefiting from large-scale synthetic training data, have demonstrated strong generalization. However, they often hallucinate depth on non-Lambertian surfaces, estimating reflected content in mirrors or transmitted content behind glass rather than the physical surface itself. Adapting these models with real-world data is challenging because conventional depth sensors are also unreliable in such regions. We observe that while the appearance of a non-Lambertian surface varies with its reflected or transmitted environment, its underlying geometry remains unchanged. Based on this observation, we propose GIFT (Geometry-Invariant Fine-Tuning), a parameter-efficient post-training framework that requires no measured depth labels. We collect groups of RGB images under controlled appearance changes while keeping the camera and target geometry fixed. GIFT exploits geometric invariance across these observations to suppress non-Lambertian depth hallucinations while retaining general depth estimation capability. We further construct a controlled benchmark that evaluates non-Lambertian depth recovery, robustness to appearance changes, and performance retention in other regions. Experiments on our benchmark and an independent real-world dataset demonstrate that GIFT improves depth prediction for mirrors and transparent objects while largely preserving the base model's performance, providing a practical and low-cost approach for adapting monocular depth foundation models to non-Lambertian scenes.

</details>


### [221] [STEAM:ASpatio-TEmporal Alignment Mixture-of-Experts Model with Hierarchical Pre-training for EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02070)
*Zhu Chen,Dingkun Liu,Yuheng Chen,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STEAM，一个用于EEG基础模型的分层迁移框架，通过双分支时空编码器和共享软混合专家（SSMoE）模块实现通用表示学习与范式特定特化的统一，在七个下游数据集和十四个评估设置中取得最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经信号解码算法泛化能力有限且适配成本高，现有的BCI基础模型难以同时实现通用可迁移性、准确解码和高效下游适配。

Method: 提出STEAM分层迁移框架，采用双分支时空编码器，其中共享软混合专家（SSMoE）模块对齐空间和时间分支，通过紧凑的软槽集合促进互补表征间的信息交换。构建分层预训练策略，基于Stage-I通用初始化进一步将模型特化到目标范式，无需从头重新训练。

Result: 在七个下游数据集和十四个评估设置中，STEAM在比较方法中获得最佳平均排名，且推理成本（FLOPs）具有竞争力。分层预训练策略在范式特定解码精度上获得一致的提升。

Conclusion: STEAM成功融合了通用表示学习与范式特定特化，验证了分层迁移框架在BCI基础模型中的有效性，为解决泛化性与专门化之间的权衡提供了新思路。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) have been widely used in motor rehabilitation, disease diagnosis, and other neural engineering scenarios. However, conventional neural signal decoding algorithms often suffer from limited generalizability and high adaptation costs, motivating recent interest in BCI foundation models. Existing approaches still struggle to jointly achieve general transferability, accurate decoding, and efficient downstream adaptation. We present STEAM, a hierarchical transfer framework that reconciles general-purpose representation learning with paradigm-specific specialization in EEG foundation models. The framework is instantiated as a dual-branch spatio-temporal encoder in which a shared soft mixture-of-experts (SSMoE) module aligns the spatial and temporal branches, allowing complementary representations to exchange information through a compact set of soft slots. Across seven downstream datasets and fourteen evaluation settings, STEAM attains the best average rank among the compared methods at a competitive inference cost measured in FLOPs. Building upon the Stage-I general initialization, the hierarchical pre-training strategy further specializes the model to a target paradigm without retraining from scratch, yielding consistent gains in paradigm-specific decoding accuracy.

</details>


### [222] [Deep Multimodal Fusion Detection through Spatial Mask and Channel Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02092)
*Guandi Wang,Ming Li,Yunsen Xing,Junle Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力驱动的互补重采样框架（Attention-Driven Complementarity Resampling, ADCR），用于提升跨模态目标检测的鲁棒性。通过语义掩码交换和可学习的通道竞争机制，克服了传统特征级融合方法在双骨干架构中对单一模态统计特性的过拟合问题，在多个数据集上取得了具有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征级融合方法主要在两模态间进行加权并在统一表示空间中融合，这会导致双骨干架构对单一模态的统计特性产生过拟合或过度专化，降低模型的泛化能力。

Method: 1）基于共享通道空间注意力机制；2）引入语义掩码交换（Semantic Mask Exchange），在训练阶段主动混合模态边界，迫使骨干网络学习不依赖固定模态标签的通用特征；3）提出可学习通道竞争（Learnable Channel Competition），以通道级且可学习的方式对特征进行采样和聚合。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法有效且在与现有最先进方法的比较中取得了具有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效缓解单模态过拟合问题，提升跨模态目标检测的鲁棒性，代码已作为补充材料提供。

Abstract: Deep multimodal fusion for object detection has demonstrated good performance through mining modal characteristics. However, existing feature-level fusion methods mainly weigh between two modalities and unify them in a unified representation space. This can lead to overfitting or over-specialization of the statistical properties of a single modality within a dual-backbone architecture. This paper proposes an Attention-Driven Complementarity Resampling framework for robust improvement of cross-modality object detection. Based on a shared channel spatial attention mechanism, we first introduce the semantic mask exchange to actively mix the boundaries of the modalities during the training phase, forcing the backbone network to learn generalized features without relying on fixed modal labels. Then we propose a learnable channel competition to sample and aggregate features in a channel-wise and learnable way. Our experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that the proposed method is effective and yields competitive results among existing state-of-the-art approaches. The source code is provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [223] [Same Semantics, Different Paths: Self-Improving Alignment for Vision-Text Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02109)
*Tianyu Liang,Xiangxi Zheng,Yilin Wang,Dongxing Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPIRAL框架，通过自监督对齐解决视觉文本压缩（VTC）中渲染图像表示与原生文本表示之间的跨路径不一致问题，无需外部教师或额外标注，即可显著提升VTC性能并逼近原生文本输入效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VTC将长文本渲染为图像并用ViT编码，但ViT在自然图像上预训练，捕获的是视觉属性而非语言语义，导致渲染图像表示与原生文本表示存在"跨路径不一致"（cross-path inconsistency），这是VTC中被忽视的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出SPIRAL框架，采用自监督对齐方案，仅利用模型自身的文本路径行为作为监督。包含两个互补粒度的模块：(1) token级在线策略蒸馏（OPD）保证局部忠实性；(2) 序列级DPO偏好优化保证全局连贯性。

Result: 在VTCBench上，SPIRAL将Qwen3-VL-8B的总体得分从35.10提升至54.02，接近原生文本输入性能（55.60），超越高达30倍规模的模型。OPD擅长检索且样本高效，DPO在推理和记忆上更强且随数据规模扩展更好。泛化能力在域外基准上也得到验证。

Conclusion: 有效VTC的关键在于将渲染图像表示对齐回原生文本语义，SPIRAL通过自监督方式成功弥合了这一差距，无需外部监督即可实现接近原生文本输入的性能。

Abstract: Vision-Text Compression (VTC) renders long texts into images and encodes them through the vision encoder (ViT), compressing thousands of text tokens into far fewer visual tokens. However, since the ViT is pretrained predominantly on natural images, it captures visual attributes (glyphs, font sizes, layout) rather than linguistic semantics, causing rendered-image representations to diverge from native-text representations. We term this cross-path inconsistency and show, via rendering perturbation experiments, that it is a critical yet overlooked bottleneck of VTC. We propose SPIRAL (Self-improving Path Integration and Realignment), a self-supervised alignment framework that closes this gap using only the model's own text-path behavior as supervision, requiring no external teachers or additional annotations. SPIRAL operates at two complementary granularities: token-level on-policy distillation (OPD) for local faithfulness, and sequence-level preference optimization (DPO) for global coherence. On VTCBench, SPIRAL improves the overall score of Qwen3-VL-8B from 35.10 to 54.02, approaching the native text-input performance (55.60) and outperforming models up to 30x larger. The two granularities exhibit complementary strengths: OPD excels at retrieval and is sample-efficient, while DPO is stronger on reasoning and memory and scales better with data. SPIRAL's benefits also generalize to out-of-domain benchmarks, confirming that effective VTC hinges on aligning rendered-image representations back to native-text semantics.

</details>


### [224] [HAFI-VLM: A Frequency Perspective for Diagnosing and Enhancing Visual Perception in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02124)
*Jin Cui,Chuanchang Su,Jiayi Lu,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HAFI-VLM，通过任务条件化的频率路径（HAFI）向视觉编码器注入互补的低、中、高频证据，并配合Visual Enrichment Layer Adapter校准LLM注意力，从而提升VLM在细粒度视觉任务上的表现，同时避免额外高分辨率编码的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在需要细粒度视觉证据的预测中表现不可靠。作者发现了一个被忽视的原因：光谱响应刚性——预训练视觉编码器在不同图像和任务中表现出固定的、逐层的光谱特征，微调后变化很小。由于视觉编码器只接收图像而无法感知文本查询需求，它们无法根据当前任务自适应地提取所需频率的证据。

Method: HAFI-VLM由两个核心组件构成：(1) Hierarchical Adaptive Frequency Injection (HAFI)：在多个编码器深度使用文本调制、空间对齐的交叉注意力，检索互补的低、中、高频证据；(2) Visual Enrichment Layer Adapter：重新校准浅层LLM注意力，使其有效利用增强后的视觉token。整体框架在保留预训练语义表示的同时引入任务条件化的频率路径。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5和Qwen2.5-VL上进行实验，在通用VQA、文本密集型理解和幻觉鲁棒性上均取得一致改进，性能优于表示层增强方法以及大多数基于分辨率或裁剪的方法，且无需额外的高分辨率编码。

Conclusion: 机制分析表明HAFI恢复了任务依赖的光谱分配，同时保持语义注意力。该工作确立了频率增强作为改善VLM感知能力的一条独立且有效的路径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) remain unreliable when predictions require fine-grained visual evidence. We identify a previously overlooked cause: spectral response rigidity. Despite substantial frequency variation across images and tasks, pretrained vision encoders exhibit persistent, encoder-specific layerwise spectral profiles that change only marginally under downstream fine-tuning. Since pretrained vision encoders only receive images, they cannot adapt spectral extraction to the evidence required by the current query. We therefore propose HAFI-VLM, which introduces a task-conditioned frequency pathway while preserving the pretrained semantic representation. Hierarchical Adaptive Frequency Injection (HAFI) retrieves complementary low-, mid-, and high-frequency evidence at multiple encoder depths using text-modulated, spatially aligned cross-attention. A Visual Enrichment Layer Adapter further recalibrates shallow LLM attention to effectively utilize the enriched visual tokens. Experiments on LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen2.5-VL demonstrate consistent improvements in general VQA, text-rich understanding, and hallucination robustness, outperforming representation-level enhancement methods and most resolution- or cropping-based approaches without additional high-resolution encoding. Mechanistic analyses show that HAFI restores task-dependent spectral allocation while retaining semantic attention, establishing frequency enrichment as a distinct and effective route for improving VLM perception.

</details>


### [225] [PromptPath: Prompt-Adaptive Computational Pathways for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02129)
*Hangrui Zhang,Feifei Shao,Yawei Luo,Ping Liu,Jiaxiang Liu,Zuoqi Tang,Zhao Wang,Hongwei Wang,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PromptPath，一种自适应的上下文学习（ICL）框架，通过提示驱动的动态路由机制选择性地激活轻量级低秩专家，形成针对不同提示的任务特化计算通路，从而在3D点云和2D视觉识别基准上超越现有ICL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL方法存在浅层任务适应问题：提示仅作为语义上下文线索隐式推断任务意图，底层计算过程未改变，限制了任务特化适应和推理可解释性。作者认为提示不仅应调节特征表示，还应动态调节模型的计算路径。

Method: 提出PromptPath框架：(1) 引入提示驱动的路由机制，选择性激活和组合轻量级低秩专家；(2) 将提示信息直接整合到推理过程中；(3) 根据不同提示形成任务特化的计算通路，动态重构模型计算以增强任务特化和可解释性。

Result: 在3D点云和2D视觉识别基准上的大量实验表明，PromptPath一致优于最先进的ICL基线，并展现出强大的跨领域和跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过让提示不仅影响特征表示还动态调控模型的计算路径，PromptPath实现了计算层面的任务适应，显著提升了ICL的任务特化能力和推理可解释性。
