Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) has attracted increasing attention for enabling models to perform new tasks using only a few ``input--output'' prompt examples. However, existing approaches suffer from \textbf{shallow task adaptation}, where prompts are primarily used as contextual cues to implicitly infer task intent through semantic representations, while the underlying computational process remains unchanged. This limitation restricts task-specific adaptation and compromises inference interpretability. We argue that prompts should not only condition feature representations but also dynamically regulate the model's computation pathways. To this end, we propose \textbf{PromptPath}, an adaptive ICL framework that enables computation-level adaptation through prompt-conditioned dynamic pathways. Specifically, PromptPath introduces a prompt-driven routing mechanism to selectively activate and compose lightweight low-rank experts, forming task-specific computational pathways tailored to different prompts. By integrating prompt information directly into the inference process, PromptPath dynamically reconfigures model computation to enhance task specialization and interpretability. Extensive experiments on 3D point cloud and 2D visual recognition benchmarks demonstrate that PromptPath consistently outperforms state-of-the-art ICL baselines while exhibiting strong cross-domain and cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [226] [Messages, Not Tokens: Grounded Coresets for Faithful VLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02134)
*Long Qian,Jiaqi Wei,Bingke Zhu,Yingying Chen,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为GMC（Grounded Message Coreset Pruning）的训练无关视觉token压缩方法，通过构建解码器消息的紧凑coreset来保持视觉信息保真度，相比现有Top-K剪枝方法在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上保留97.78%能力的同时减少80.2%的视觉token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分数的视觉token压缩方法为每个token分配独立的显著性分数并保留Top-K，但忽略了文本查询会消费来自视觉群体的集体、带符号的注意力消息，而并非孤立的patches。这导致同等大小的Top-K集合可能重复覆盖单一显著区域，遗漏稀疏但互补的证据，并丢弃被移除群体携带的信息。

Method: 将忠实视觉压缩形式化为构建解码器消息的紧凑coreset，提出训练无关的GMC方法。该方法联合在查询接地、外观和坐标感知证据之间分配支持，然后将被丢弃的状态传输到选定的代表token上，并保留其原始多模态位置，再进行物理压缩和原生注意力恢复。方法分解为两个耦合组件：选择覆盖所需消息模式的载体，以及在载体上实现带符号的群体消息。同时推导了连接组件误差与带符号消息失真、视觉创新和候选边缘稳定性的边界。

Result: 在多个VLM家族和多样化基准上的实验表明，GMC-H2在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上保留97.78%的全量相对平均能力，同时减少80.2%的视觉token；GMC-L16达到100.36%。对照实验验证了集体支持和群体实现共同驱动了这些增益。

Conclusion: GMC通过将视觉压缩重新定义为解码器消息的coreset构建，有效解决了Top-K剪枝导致的集体信息丢失问题，在保留显著区域的同时也能覆盖互补证据和被移除群体携带的信息，实现了更忠实的视觉压缩。

Abstract: Modern vision language models (VLMs) turn high-resolution images into long sequences of visual tokens. Every token traverses the language decoder and persists in its prompt KV cache, inflating inference cost and motivating aggressive visual compression. Existing score-based methods assign each token an independent importance score and retain the Top-K. However, text queries consume collective, signed attention messages from the visual population, not isolated patches. Consequently, equally sized Top-K sets can repeatedly cover one salient region, omit sparse but complementary evidence and discard information carried by the removed population. We therefore formulate faithful visual compression as constructing a compact coreset for decoder messages, and introduce our training-free Grounded Message Coreset Pruning (GMC) which jointly allocates support across query-grounded, appearance, and coordinate-aware evidence, then transports discarded states into selected representatives at their original multimodal positions before physical compaction and native attention resume. This decomposes faithful compression into two coupled components, including selecting carriers that cover the required message modes and realizing the signed population message on those carriers. We further derive bounds connecting their errors to signed-message distortion, visual innovation, and candidate-margin stability. Experiments across multiple VLM families and diverse benchmarks demonstrate strong performance, with GMC-H2 retaining 97.78% Full-relative mean capability on Qwen2.5-VL-7B using 80.2% fewer visual tokens, while GMC-L16 reaches 100.36%. Controlled interventions verify that collective support and population realization jointly drive these gains.

</details>


### [227] [Two Sides of the Same Coin: Co-Evolving Search for Cross-Task Attacks on Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02137)
*Xuanhui Lin,Junhao Dong,Mingrong Gong,Yucheng Chen,Xinghua Qu,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于进化计算引导的跨模态攻击框架，用于统一视觉语言模型（VLMs）。该框架在文本侧演化硬负样本语义嵌入，在视觉侧结合动量梯度更新与进化选择、变异、交叉操作来搜索对抗扰动，联合优化以生成跨任务可迁移的对抗样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对VLMs的攻击方法通常采用单轨迹梯度优化或任务特定目标，限制了搜索空间的探索能力，导致跨任务可迁移性较差。

Method: 提出进化计算引导的跨模态攻击框架：(1) 文本侧演化硬负语义嵌入提供跨模态排斥；(2) 视觉侧维护目标区域扰动种群，结合动量梯度与进化操作（选择、变异、交叉）探索多条可行轨迹；(3) 联合优化语义负向引导和局部扰动，使源对象语义一致地转向目标类别。

Result: 在Florence-2、OFA和UnifiedIO-2上验证了该框架在图像描述、目标检测、区域分类和目标定位等任务上的强攻击性能。消融实验证实了文本侧语义演化与图像侧扰动演化的互补有效性，并展示了框架的高效性和跨任务可迁移性。理论分析证明协同进化搜索保持扰动可行性、防止最佳适应度退化，并相比单轨迹优化提高了到达高边界对抗区域的概率。

Conclusion: 进化计算引导的协同跨模态搜索策略能有效提升对统一VLMs的攻击效果，兼具理论保证和实证验证，为多模态对抗攻击提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit strong generalization across multimodal tasks but remain vulnerable to adversarial perturbations. Existing attacks typically follow single-trajectory gradient optimization or task-specific objectives, limiting search-space exploration and cross-task transferability. We propose an evolutionary-computation-guided cross-modal attack framework for unified VLMs. The framework adaptively searches both textual and visual spaces. On the textual side, it evolves hard negative semantic embeddings around the source-category representation to provide diverse cross-modal repulsion. On the visual side, it maintains a population of object-region perturbations and combines momentum-based gradient updates with evolutionary selection, mutation, and crossover to more reliably explore multiple feasible trajectories. Jointly optimizing semantic negative guidance and localized perturbations generates adversarial examples that consistently shift source-object semantics toward target categories across vision-language tasks. Theoretical analyses show that the co-evolutionary search preserves perturbation feasibility, prevents degradation of the best observed fitness, and increases the probability of reaching high-margin adversarial regions compared with single-trajectory optimization. Experiments on Florence-2, OFA, and UnifiedIO-2 demonstrate strong overall attack performance across image captioning, object detection, region categorization, and object localization. Ablation studies further verify the complementary effectiveness of text-side semantic evolution and image-side perturbation evolution, as well as the framework's efficiency and cross-task transferability.

</details>


### [228] [HiResNets: Native Full-HD Video Recognition with Foveal Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02140)
*Shivani Mall,Swarnim Jain,Joao F. Henriques*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HiResNets，一种利用残差网络的残差流作为高分辨率缓冲区、通过log-polar图像扭曲操作实现对数平方复杂度增长的卷积网络架构，能够高效处理超高分辨率视频，在自我中心视频识别任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像和视频识别模型的进步伴随着内存和计算开销的急剧增长，尤其是基于网格采样的卷积网络和视觉Transformer中，内存和计算随图像分辨率呈二次方（或更高）增长，这严重限制了高分辨率视频处理的能力。

Method: 利用残差架构的残差流作为高分辨率缓冲区，卷积块仅通过log-polar图像扭曲操作进行读写。各层自适应地聚焦于帧的不同部分，仅在焦点附近保持高分辨率，完整的高分辨率表示在残差流中逐步构建，类比生物视觉中的扫视机制。同时提出理论构造，消除残差流分辨率上的二次依赖关系。

Result: 实验表明HiResNets能够学习类似人类视觉的注视（foveate）行为，在困难的自我中心视频识别任务中表现优于现有方法，尤其是在包含小物体和细粒度识别的自我中心视频场景下。

Conclusion: 通过模拟生物视觉的注视+扫视机制，可以显著降低高分辨率视频处理的复杂度，并提升特定场景下的识别性能，为高效视频理解提供了新思路。

Abstract: Much of the recent progress in image and video recognition has come at the cost of memory: larger models, increased resolution, and longer temporal contexts. An inevitable component is the quadratic (or larger) growth of memory and compute based on image resolution, which is a property of the grid sampling used in convolutional networks and vision transformers. In this work we study residual networks whose convolutional blocks have logarithmic-square growth instead, enabling them to process very high-resolution video quickly. The key insight is to use a residual architecture's residual stream as a high-resolution buffer, to which convolutional blocks only read and write via log-polar image warp operations. Layers adaptively focus on different parts of each frame, with very high resolution only near the focus point. A complete high-resolution representation is built up in the residual stream, analogous to eye saccades creating a complete picture in biological vision, and a theoretical construction is presented that eliminates the quadratic dependency of the residual stream resolution. Experiments demonstrate that our proposed HiResNets learn to foveate around scenes similarly to human vision, and have superior performance in difficult egocentric video recognition tasks, especially egocentric video with small objects and fine-grained recognition.

</details>


### [229] [Quaternion Tensor Modeling for Joint Color-Polarization Demosaicking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02144)
*Yanqing Song,Jifei Miao,Chaoqian Li,Rui Mei,Kit Ian Kou,Liqiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于四元数张量结合Stokes域全变分正则化的彩色偏振去马赛克（CPDM）方法，将0°、45°、90°、135°四个偏振方向的图像编码为三阶四元数张量，利用低秩先验捕捉全局结构冗余，并通过正交变换将空间梯度映射到Stokes域进行分量自适应正则化，在实验中取得了优于现有方法的去马赛克性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分焦平面（DoFP）彩色偏振相机可以快照采集彩色偏振马赛克图像，但稀疏采样模式使彩色偏振去马赛克问题严重不适定。现有方法未能联合利用偏振通道间的相关性以及偏振成像固有的物理约束，导致明显的去马赛克伪影。

Method: 1) 通过相关性分析发现偏振通道间的相关性强于颜色通道间；2) 将四个偏振方向的彩色偏振图像编码为三阶四元数张量的四个分量，颜色通道沿第三模态排列；3) 对四元数张量施加低秩先验以利用全局结构冗余；4) 通过正交变换将空间梯度映射到Stokes域，分离强度、偏振和残差变化，并使用自适应四元数权重实现分量特定正则化；5) 推导了高效的优化算法求解模型。

Result: 大量实验证明该方法在彩色偏振去马赛克性能上优于现有方法，能够有效减少伪影并保持Stokes向量的能量一致性。

Conclusion: 通过四元数张量建模结合Stokes域TV正则化，该方法成功联合利用了偏振通道间的强相关性和偏振成像的物理约束，为彩色偏振去马赛克提供了一种更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Division-of-focal-plane (DoFP) color polarization cameras enable snapshot acquisition of color polarization mosaic images, but the inherently sparse sampling pattern makes color polarization demosaicking severely ill-posed. Existing methods often fail to jointly exploit the correlations among polarization channels and the physical constraints inherent in polarization imaging, resulting in noticeable demosaicking artifacts. To address this issue, a quaternion-tensor-based color polarization demosaicking (CPDM) method incorporating Stokes-domain total variation (TV) regularization is proposed. Correlation analysis shows that the correlations among polarization channels are stronger than those among color channels. Accordingly, the color polarization images acquired at $0^\circ$, $45^\circ$, $90^\circ$, and $135^\circ$ are encoded into the four components of a third-order quaternion tensor, with the color channels organized along its third mode. A low-rank prior is then imposed on the quaternion tensor to exploit the global structural redundancy in the color polarization data. Moreover, spatial gradients are mapped to the Stokes domain through an orthogonal transformation to separate intensity, polarization and residual variations, with adaptive quaternion weights enabling component-specific regularization and preserving the energy consistency of the reconstructed Stokes vectors. An efficient optimization algorithm is derived for the resulting model. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior demosaicking performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [230] [UniqueSplat: View-conditioned 3D Gaussian Splatting for Generalizable 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02145)
*Haixu Song,Xiaoke Yang,Shengjun Zhang,Jiwen Lu,Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniqueSplat，一种基于视图条件的端到端前馈3D高斯泼溅模型，能够针对每个视图查询重建定制化的3D辐射场。它通过双分支视图条件超网络，同时学习视图无关嵌入和视图特定知识，使高斯分布能根据视角动态调整，在多个数据集上超越现有方法并展现出强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前馈方法（如pixelSplat和MVSplat）为每个场景生成固定的高斯分布，在预测高斯时未纳入目标视图信息，导致其渲染的图像缺乏对特定视点的适应能力。

Method: 提出双分支视图条件超网络：一个分支学习视图无关的嵌入（探索多视图间可共享的知识），另一个分支学习视图特定知识（在测试时使模型适应特定视图）。视图条件信息作为先验被融入网络参数中，使高斯分布能够根据视图动态调整。

Result: 在RealEstate10K、ACID和DTU数据集上均优于现有最先进方法，且在跨数据集评估中表现更优，展现了显著的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将视图条件信息作为先验融入网络参数，UniqueSplat成功实现了对特定视图的自适应3D重建，验证了视图条件化策略在3D高斯泼溅重建中的有效性和泛化优势。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose UniqueSplat, a view-conditioned feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting model to reconstruct customized 3D radiance fields for each view query. Existing feed-forward methods such as pixelSplat and MVSplat aim to generate fixed Gaussians across all views of each scene by minimizing the error between rendered views and ground-truth images. However, such fixed Gaussians generally render images from all views and lack the ability to adapt to specific viewpoints, as they do not incorporate target view information when predicting Gaussians. To address this, our UniqueSplat learns the view-conditioned information as a prior and incorporates this knowledge into network parameters, so that Gaussians are dynamically adjusted in accordance with different views. Specifically, we propose a two-branch view-conditioned hyperNetwork to simultaneously learn view-agnostic embeddings and view-specific knowledge, which not only explores the shareable knowledge from various views, but also adapts the model to specific views at test time. Extensive experiments on widely-used datasets including RealEstate10K, ACID and DTU demonstrate the superiority of UniqueSplat over the state-of-the-art methods. Moreover, UniqueSplat encouragingly outperforms existing methods in cross-dataset evaluation, showing its notable generalization ability.

</details>


### [231] [PhyCheck: Fine-Grained Evidence-Grounded Dataset for Physical Law Understanding in Video-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02150)
*Zhongjie Ba,Shengwang Xu,Peng Cheng,Jinyang Zou,Ting Yu,Zhibo Wang,Zhan Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyCheck，一个用于评估和改进视频大语言模型（VideoLLMs）物理定律理解能力的视频问答数据集，包含粗粒度（判断物理一致性）、细粒度（捕捉物理细节）和诊断性（外部因果背景）三个子集，实验表明训练可提升物理一致性理解，但模型仍难以整合外部因果条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频语言模型在通用视频理解任务上表现强劲，但难以可靠判断观察事件是否符合物理定律。现有基准主要评估生成视频的物理质量，缺乏系统评估和改进VideoLLMs物理定律理解的基准。

Method: 构建PhyCheck数据集，包含三个子集：（1）粗粒度子集：判断视频现象是否符合或违反物理定律；（2）细粒度子集：检验模型能否捕捉导致违反或符合的物理细节；（3）诊断性子集：提供外部因果背景，评估模型能否据此重新调整判断。使用这些子集作为结构化监督对Qwen2.5-VL进行微调。

Result: 微调后的Qwen2.5-VL在物理一致性理解上有显著提升，但在诊断性子集上的评估显示当前模型仍难以将额外因果条件融入决策过程。

Conclusion: 研究发现表面层级的不一致识别与深层物理机制理解之间存在差距，PhyCheck为评估和改进VideoLLMs的物理理解能力提供了基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence and world models require video understanding systems to go beyond recognizing objects and actions and develop an understanding of physical regularities. However, despite their strong performance on general video understanding tasks, current video-language models still struggle to reliably determine whether an observed event conforms to specific physical laws. Existing benchmarks primarily assess the physical quality of generated videos, providing limited support for systematically evaluating and improving the physical-law understanding of Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs). To address this gap, we introduce PhyCheck, a video question answering dataset organized at two complementary levels of granularity. The coarse-grained subset asks models to determine whether the phenomenon shown in a video conforms to or violates physical laws, while the fine-grained subset further examines whether models can capture physical details responsible for the violation or compliance. We use these subsets as structured supervision to improve physical understanding. In addition, the dataset contains a diagnostic subset with external causal context that reveal hidden factors affecting physical plausibility, assessing whether models can recalibrate their judgments accordingly. Experiments with Fine-tune Qwen2.5-VL show that training with the proposed data substantially improves the understanding of physical-consistency, while evaluations in the diagnostic subset reveal that current models still have difficulty incorporating additional causal conditions into their decisions. These findings highlight the gap between recognizing surface-level inconsistencies and understanding underlying physical mechanisms, and provide a foundation for evaluating and improving physical understanding in Video-LLMs.

</details>


### [232] [AdaForensics: Learning A Characteristic-aware Adaptive Deepfake Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02160)
*Xiaoke Yang,Haixu Song,Xiangyu Lu,Shao-Lun Huang,Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaForensics，一种特征感知的自适应深度伪造检测网络。与现有使用固定网络的方法不同，AdaForensics通过双分支超网络动态调整检测器参数，同时学习特征无关和特征特定的嵌入，为不同个体提供定制化的伪造检测，在多个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测方法使用固定的网络架构对所有图像采用同一检测器，忽略了不同人脸的个体特征差异，无法为不同个体提供定制化的伪造检测服务，导致检测性能受限。

Method: 提出AdaForensics，采用双分支HyperNetwork架构：一分支学习特征无关（characteristic-agnostic）的可共享抽象特征，另一分支学习特征特定（characteristic-specific）的嵌入；超网络根据输入人脸的特征动态调整检测器参数，实现测试时的自适应检测。

Result: 在FaceForensics、Celeb-DF和DFDC三个广泛使用的基准数据集上，AdaForensics均超越了现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过动态自适应机制，根据个体面部特征定制化调整检测器参数，能够更有效地检测深度伪造内容，证明了特征感知的自适应检测框架的优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a characteristic-aware adaptive network named AdaForensics for deepfake detection. Most existing methods learn a fixed network to detect deepfakes based on carefully-designed network architectures. However, these methods employ the same deepfake detector for all the images despite of various facial characteristic, which fail to provide customized forgery detection for different individuals. To address this, our AdaForensics simultaneously learns characteristic-agnostic and characteristic-specific embeddings, where the detector dynamically adapts to varying faces with our designed hypernetwork on the fly. More specifically, our AdaForensics not only explores the shareable abstractions from various deepfake images, but also adapts the detector to the given characteristic at test time. To achieve this, we propose a two-branch HyperNetwork to learn an adaptive deepfake detector, which automatically adjusts the parameters based on characteristic of the input. Extensive experiments on widely-used datasets including FaceForensics, Celeb-DF and DFDC demonstrate our AdaForensics outperforms the state-of-the-art works.

</details>


### [233] [GSRAIN: Physically Calibrated High-/Low-Frequency Rainfall Synthesis for 3D Gaussian Driving Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02177)
*Fanyu Wang,Longgao Zhang,Junyi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GSRAIN是一种面向3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）自动驾驶场景的高/低频降雨合成方法。它结合实测降雨数据构建的高频雨滴模型和几何感知单步扩散模型生成的低频雨天外观，在统一的3DGS场景中融合两种效果，支持0-13mm/h的降雨强度控制。实验表明其FID达149.09，优于CycleGAN-Turbo和WeatherEdit，并能揭示算法在不同降雨条件下的场景依赖性能变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶降雨仿真方法在物理可控性和多视角一致性方面存在局限，难以构建可重复、可闭环测试的雨天场景。

Method: GSRAIN包含两个核心组件：1）基于实测降雨数据构建的高频雨滴模型，用于模拟雨滴的物理细节；2）采用几何感知的单步扩散模型生成低频雨天外观。两者在统一的3DGS场景中融合，实现0-13mm/h范围的降雨强度连续控制。

Result: GSRAIN在FID指标上达到149.09，优于CycleGAN-Turbo（155.71）和WeatherEdit（157.94）。目标检测和闭环驾驶实验进一步验证了生成的场景能揭示被评估算法在不同降雨条件下的场景依赖性能变化。

Conclusion: GSRAIN为自动驾驶提供了一种物理可控、可重复且兼容闭环仿真的雨天测试场景构建方法，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Existing rainfall simulation methods for autonomous driving remain limited in physical controllability and multi-view consistency. This paper presents GSRAIN, a high-/low-frequency rainfall synthesis method for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) driving scenes. GSRAIN constructs a high-frequency raindrop model from measured rainfall data and generates low-frequency rainy appearance using a geometry-aware single-step diffusion model. The two effects are then fused in a unified 3DGS scene, enabling rainfall-intensity control over the range of 0--13~mm/h. The proposed method achieves a Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) of 149.09, outperforming CycleGAN-Turbo (155.71) and WeatherEdit (157.94). Object-detection and closed-loop driving experiments further show that the generated scenes expose scene-dependent performance changes of the evaluated algorithms under controllable rainfall. These results indicate that GSRAIN provides an effective approach for constructing physically controllable, repeatable, and closed-loop-compatible rainy-weather test scenes for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [234] [SWINSleepNet: A Hierarchical Context-Aware Framework for Sleep Staging (v2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02183)
*Chongjian Wang,Junjie Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SwinSleepNet，一个层次化上下文感知双流框架，分别优化睡眠分期的epoch内表征学习和epoch间上下文建模。时间域分支使用卷积编码器捕获细粒度波形细节，时间-频率分支使用Swin Transformer提取局部频谱时空特征和长距离空间依赖，通过双向上下文模块融合跨epoch时序依赖进行分类。在Sleep-EDF-20、Sleep-EDF-78和SHHS数据集上验证了方法的有效性，尤其在困难的N1阶段和过渡epoch上表现出更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动睡眠分期方法在模糊和过渡性睡眠阶段（尤其N1期）表现不佳，原因是缺乏对epoch内细粒度结构和跨区域频谱复杂依赖的充分建模。传统epoch级编码器难以提取细微的时间微结构和epoch内跨区域交互，导致困难类别的识别准确率不理想。

Method: 提出SwinSleepNet双流框架：(1) 时间域分支采用卷积编码器捕获原始EEG信号的精细波形时间细节；(2) 时间-频率分支使用Swin Transformer提取局部频谱时空特征、层次化多尺度信息和长距离空间依赖；(3) 将多分支特征融合为集成嵌入；(4) 通过双向上下文模块捕获跨epoch时序依赖，用于最终睡眠阶段分类。

Result: 在Sleep-EDF-20、Sleep-EDF-78和SHHS三个公开数据集上的综合实验验证了该方法具有竞争力的整体性能，在困难的N1阶段和过渡epoch上表现出更强的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 基于层次化架构优化的epoch内表征学习对自动睡眠分期任务有显著帮助，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Automatic sleep staging is a critical role in sleep disorder diagnosis, sleep quality assessment, and long-term health monitoring; however, existing approaches suffer poor performance on ambiguous and transition-related sleep stages, caused by inadequate modeling of fine-grained intra-epoch structures and complex cross-region spectral dependencies. Traditional epoch-level encoders commonly fail to extract subtle temporal microstructures and intra-epoch cross-region interactions, resulting in unsatisfactory recognition accuracy for hard categories such as the N1 stage. To tackle these drawbacks, we propose SwinSleepNet, a hierarchical context-aware dual-stream framework that separately optimizes intra-epoch representation learning and inter-epoch contextual modeling. Concretely, we characterize each sleep epoch from two complementary perspectives: raw time-domain EEG signal and its time-frequency transformation. The time-domain branch adopts convolutional encoders to capture fine waveform temporal details, and the time-frequency branch uses Swin Transformer to extract local spectro-temporal features, hierarchical multi-scale information and long-range spatial dependencies. The multi-branch extracted features are fused into integrated embeddings, which are optimized by a bidirectional context module to capture cross-epoch temporal dependencies for final sleep stage classification. Comprehensive experiments on Sleep-EDF-20, Sleep-EDF-78 and SHHS datasets verify that our method achieves competitive overall performance, and exhibits stronger robustness and stability on difficult N1 stages and transitional epochs. The results prove that optimized intra-epoch representation learning based on hierarchical architecture greatly benefits automatic sleep staging tasks.

</details>


### [235] [SPIRIT: Spatio-temporal Pairwise Relational Modeling of Instrument-Tissue Interactions for Surgical Action Triplet Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02188)
*Saurav Sharma,Lorenzo Arboit,Nabani Banik,Sarah Meuli,Julia Alekseenko,Jan Liechti,Franziska Heitzinger,Michela Orsi,Didier Mutter,Daniel Gero,Philipp C. Nett,Beat P. Muller,Joel L. Lavanchy,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPIRIT是一个用于外科手术动作三元组识别的结构化框架，通过分别学习器械、动词和目标的时空表示，建模两两关系并组合成三元组预测，配合多头蒸馏稳定训练。作者还建立了跨四个地理中心的多中心数据集MultiBypass-4C-T40，用于评估跨中心迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科手术中细粒度的动作理解对手术室情境感知辅助至关重要（安全监控、不良事件识别、技能评估）。现有方法将手术动作三元组<器械,动词,目标>视为扁平类别标签，但不同医疗中心在采集条件、外科医生风格、工具使用和组织处理上存在差异，导致跨中心泛化不可靠。同时，现有数据集不支持明确的中心间迁移评估，这是亟待解决的关键问题。

Method: SPIRIT框架采用结构化分解策略：1) 首先学习器械、动词和目标各自独立的时空表示；2) 建模它们之间的两两关系（pairwise relations）；3) 将关系组合成连贯的三元组预测；4) 使用多头蒸馏（multi-head distillation）稳定学习过程。此外，构建了MultiBypass-4C-T40多中心数据集，包含四个地理分布不同的医疗中心的Roux-en-Y胃旁路手术视频，并附有阶段和步骤的辅助标注。

Result: 在多种评估协议下，SPIRIT一致性优于近期强基线方法，证明了显式关系推理（而非扁平分类）对多中心三元组识别的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模三元组内部结构关系（而非视为扁平标签）能够有效提升跨中心手术动作识别的迁移可靠性，为多中心手术视频理解提供了新的框架和评估基准。

Abstract: Fine-grained understanding of surgical activity is essential for context-aware assistance in the operating room, including safety monitoring, adverse event identification, and skill assessment. Surgical action triplets, defined as tuples of the form <instrument, verb, target>, provide a structured description of instrument-tissue interactions. A key open problem, however, is how to learn triplet representations that remain reliable across institutions, where surgical video varies in acquisition conditions, surgeon style, tool usage, and tissue handling, while existing triplet datasets do not support explicit evaluation of center-wise transfer. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{SPIRIT}, a structured framework for surgical action triplet recognition designed to learn interaction representations that transfer more reliably across centers. Instead of treating each triplet as a flat class label, SPIRIT first learns spatio-temporal representations for instruments, verbs, and targets, then models their pairwise relations, and finally composes them into coherent triplet predictions, with multi-head distillation used to stabilize learning. To evaluate this setting, we establish \textbf{MultiBypass-4C-T40}, a multi-centric dataset for dense surgical action triplet recognition in Roux-en-Y gastric bypass across four geographically distinct centers, with auxiliary phase and step annotations. Across multiple evaluation protocols, SPIRIT consistently outperforms strong recent baselines, highlighting the value of explicit relational reasoning for multi-centric triplet recognition. Code will be available at https://github.com/CAMMA-public/multibypass-4c-t40.

</details>


### [236] [DerainSplat: Feed-Forward Clean 3D Gaussian Splatting from Sparse Rainy Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02191)
*Fuzhen Jiang,Changyue Shi,Chuxiao Yang,Xinyuan Hu,Wenjie Ye,Minghao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DerainSplat是一个前馈式三维重建框架，能够从少量雨景视图直接重建干净的3D场景。它通过合成多视角除雨数据集、预测天气因子并生成支撑图来调制跨视角匹配和外观融合，最终在多个数据集上取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像去雨方法主要针对2D图像恢复，而随着具身智能和自动驾驶等空间智能应用的发展，从稀疏雨景视图前馈式重建干净3D场景变得日益重要。然而现有的前馈式3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）方法通常假设输入是干净的，在雨天条件下会失效。

Method: 1) 构建了一个大规模多视角除雨数据集，通过四阶段合成管线依次建模阴天光照、深度相关雾霾、雨线和镜头雨滴，并产生特权天气因子。2) 提出天气网络从雨景上下文中预测天气因子，生成两个支撑图：场景支撑图调制跨视角代价体匹配，辐射支撑图驱动深度对齐的外观融合以填补被损坏的像素。3) 利用导出的几何证据衰减高斯不透明度以减少虚假结构。4) 采用雨景循环一致性机制，使用预测因子重新渲染干净视图并与雨景输入对齐。

Result: 在RealEstate10K、ACID、Mip-NeRF360以及真实世界雨景等多个数据集上，DerainSplat均优于现有方法，并展现出强大的跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: DerainSplat首次实现了从稀疏雨景视图直接前馈式重建干净3D场景，通过天气因子预测和双支撑图调制有效解决了雨天条件下的3D重建难题，为空间智能应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Although image deraining has advanced substantially, existing methods mainly focus on 2D image restoration. As spatial intelligence applications such as embodied AI and autonomous driving continue to emerge, reconstructing clean 3D scenes from sparse rainy views in a feed-forward manner becomes increasingly important. Existing feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods often assume clean inputs and collapse under rainy conditions. To this end, we present \textbf{\textit{DerainSplat}}, a feed-forward framework that reconstructs clean 3D scenes from only a few rainy views. To support this task, we build a large-scale multi-view derain dataset through a four-stage synthesis pipeline that sequentially models overcast illumination, depth-dependent haze, rain streaks, and lens raindrops, producing privileged weather factors. We introduce a weather net that predicts the weather factors from rainy context and yields two support maps. Scene support modulates cross-view cost-volume matching, while radiance support drives depth-aligned appearance fusion to fill corrupted pixels. The derived geometry evidence further attenuates Gaussian opacity to reduce spurious structures. A rainy cycle consistency re-renders clean views using the predicted factors and aligns them with rainy inputs. Extensive experiments show that \textbf{\textit{DerainSplat}} outperforms existing methods on various datasets, including RealEstate10K, ACID, Mip-NeRF360, and real-world rainy scenes, with strong cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [237] [T$^2$exture: Sparsely Perturbed Thermal-to-Texture Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02192)
*Jiashuo Chen,Cheng Dai,Yanan Hu,Fanglin Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T²exture是一种稀疏扰动热纹理成像框架，通过少量主动扰动的关键帧和密集被动帧来重建时间密集的热纹理序列，仅增加0.20M参数即可在模拟基准上提升PSNR 6.66 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动长波红外（LWIR）测量在低照度下有效但缺乏精细纹理；现有方法依赖光谱感知或配准的辅助模态，数据吞吐量大且容易受到跨模态退化影响。

Method: 将热纹理定义为光源开启观测与对应光源关闭被动状态之间的残差。采用两阶段框架：第一阶段从邻近被动帧估计每个主动时刻未观测到的光源关闭被动状态，获得可靠的差分纹理锚点；第二阶段将稀疏锚点与目标时刻附近的被动结构上下文结合，重建密集序列。

Result: T²exture在模拟基准上仅添加0.20M参数到AMT-L即可提升PSNR 6.66 dB；在模拟和真实采集数据上都展示了比代表性VFI基线更清晰的纹理恢复和更强的结构保持。

Conclusion: T²exture被确立为稀疏主动采集条件下热纹理成像的实用框架。

Abstract: Thermal imaging remains effective under adverse illumination, yet passive long-wave infrared (LWIR) measurements often lack fine texture. Existing thermal texture imaging approaches commonly rely on spectral sensing or registered auxiliary modalities, incurring substantial data throughput or vulnerability to cross-modal degradation. We introduce T$^2$exture, a sparsely perturbed thermal texture imaging framework that aims to reconstruct temporally dense thermal texture sequences from densely sampled passive frames and a few actively perturbed keyframes. We define thermal texture as the residual between a source-on observation and its corresponding source-off passive state. Under sparse LWIR illumination and rapid quasi-steady paired acquisition, this residual attenuates the passive-emission background and approximates a source-induced reflected response, exposing localized material- and geometry-dependent texture. T$^2$exture reconstructs a dense sequence of this source-conditioned response through two stages. Stage 1 estimates the unobserved source-off passive state at each active instant from neighboring passive frames to obtain reliable differential texture anchors. Stage 2 combines sparse anchors with passive structural context near each target time to reconstruct the dense sequence. On the simulated benchmark, T$^2$exture adds only 0.20M parameters to AMT-L while improving PSNR by 6.66 dB. Extensive evaluations on simulated and real acquisitions further show clearer texture recovery and stronger structural preservation than representative VFI baselines. These results establish T$^2$exture as a practical framework for thermal texture imaging under sparse active acquisition.

</details>


### [238] [RSC-GestureNet: Reliability-Aware Selective Causal Recognition of Chinese Traffic Police Gestures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02200)
*Cheng Li,Renjun Gao,Boyi Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RSC-GestureNet，一种面向中国交通警察手势的可靠性感知选择性因果识别器。该模型将姿态置信度作为一等信号，在因果图推理中动态降低不可靠关节权重，并通过可靠性感知推理规则选择性输出校准预测。同时提出CTPGesture-C特征级腐败基准和RGB级诊断方法。在官方CTPGesture v1数据集上，模型达到93.33%准确率和91.71%宏F1，显著优于MD-GCN和HLP-GCN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通警察手势是自动驾驶中安全关键的感知线索。实际场景中，部署的姿态识别器需要从连续全帧视频中因果地推断指令，在手臂过渡运动中保持稳定，同时避免过度信任被污染的姿态测量结果。现有方法未能同时满足这些需求，特别是在姿态数据质量受损时缺乏可靠性建模。

Method: RSC-GestureNet包含三方面核心设计：1) 将姿态置信度作为一等信号，在图推理过程中对不可靠关节进行降权；2) 以因果方式聚合时序证据，确保在线部署可行；3) 通过可靠性感知推理规则选择性发出校准后的预测。此外，作者构建了CTPGesture-C基准，涵盖七种姿态/RGB腐败类型，并设计了RGB级诊断流程（腐败帧经MediaPipe重新处理后再识别）。

Result: 在官方CTPGesture v1完整划分（134,424个标注帧和33,451个因果窗口）上，RSC-GestureNet实现93.33±0.24%准确率、91.71±0.27%宏F1、91.69±0.29%在线宏F1、98.80±0.07% Early@10、0.153±0.013秒TTC，并获得最佳鲁棒宏F1。相比复现的MD-GCN和HLP-GCN基线，宏F1提升3.23-4.11个百分点，在线F1提升2.15-3.07个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过校准、选择性风险、统计、自适应分支和图像级重提取等多项分析，本文证明显式姿态可靠性建模能够提升交通指令识别的早期性、稳定性和鲁棒性，为自动驾驶中安全关键的交通警察手势识别提供了可靠且可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Traffic police gestures are safety-critical perception cues for autonomous driving. A deployable recognizer must infer commands causally from continuous full-frame video, remain stable around transitional arm motion, and avoid over-trusting corrupted pose measurements. This study presents RSC-GestureNet, a reliability-aware selective causal recognizer, for Chinese traffic police gestures. The model treats pose confidence as a first-class signal: unreliable joints are down weighted during graph reasoning, temporal evidence is aggregated causally, and calibrated predictions are selectively emitted through a reliability-aware inference rule. We further introduce CTPGesture-C, a reproducible feature-level corruption benchmark with seven pose/RGB degradation families, and an RGB-level diagnostic in which corrupted frames are reprocessed by MediaPipe before recognition. On the complete official CTPGesture v1 split (134,424 labeled frames and 33,451 causal windows), RSC-GestureNet achieves 93.33+-0.24% accuracy, 91.71+-0.27% macro-F1, 91.69+-0.29% online macro-F1, 98.80+-0.07% Early@10, 0.153+-0.013 s TTC, and the best robust macro-F1 among evaluated methods. Under the same split and causal protocol, it exceeds reproduced traffic-specific MD-GCN and HLP-GCN baselines by 3.23-4.11 macro-F1 points and 2.15-3.07 online-F1 points. These results, together with calibration, selective-risk, statistical, adaptive-branching, and image-level re-extraction analyses, indicate that explicit pose-reliability modeling improves early, stable, and robust traffic-command recognition.

</details>


### [239] [CLEAR: Conflict-aware Learning via Evidence-guided Adaptive Routing for Unified Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02206)
*Hantang Li,Qiang Zhu,Xiandong Meng,Debin Zhao,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLEAR是一个首个统一的单阶段框架，用于稀疏视角3D高斯泼溅超分辨率，通过证据引导的自适应路由和冲突感知优化策略，同时优化真实LR观测和外部HR先验，在合成和真实世界的4倍超分辨率基准上达到最先进的渲染质量和几何保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏视角超分辨率方法采用两阶段流水线（先LR高斯重建，再HR高斯细化），导致阶段间高斯传递和重建误差累积的问题。作者希望设计一个统一的单阶段框架来避免这种误差累积。

Method: CLEAR提出三类关键技术：(1) 高斯级冲突感知优化策略，将LR梯度视为可靠锚点，仅对严重HR冲突应用证据条件软校正；(2) 证据引导的Patch-to-Gaussian路由机制，估计patch可靠性和细节需求并将其提升到高斯空间，选择性路由高频梯度和致密化；(3) 共享高斯dropout和分离的中间训练锚定以增强训练鲁棒性。

Result: 在合成和真实世界的4倍超分辨率基准上，CLEAR一致地达到最先进的渲染质量和更优的几何保真度。

Conclusion: CLEAR通过统一的单阶段框架成功解决了稀疏视角3D高斯泼溅超分辨率的挑战，验证了冲突感知优化和证据引导路由策略在联合优化LR观测与HR先验中的有效性。

Abstract: Sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting Super-resolution is highly challenging since the sparse and low-resolution (LR) inputs lack sufficient geometric and high-frequency information for accurate reconstruction. To achieve high-quality reconstruction, existing sparse-view super-resolution methods adhere to two-stage pipeline that performs LR Gaussian reconstruction and then high-resolution (HR) Gaussian refinement, which directly results in stage-wise Gaussian transfer and reconstruction error accumulation. To this end, we propose CLEAR, a Conflict-aware Learning via Evidence-guided Adaptive Routing, as the first unified single-stage framework for Sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting Super-resolution. Specifically, CLEAR performs joint the optimization of authentic LR observations and external HR priors within a unified Gaussian representation. To mitigate the gradient conflicts introduced by sparse supervision during training, we propose a Gaussian-wise conflict-aware optimization strategy that regards the LR gradient as a reliable anchor and applies evidence-conditioned soft correction only to severe HR conflicts. Moreover, to recover high-frequency details, we introduce an evidence-guided Patch-to-Gaussian routing mechanism which estimates patch reliability and detail demand, lifts them into Gaussian space, and selectively routes high-frequency gradients and densification. Finally, we employ shared Gaussian dropout and a detached mid-training anchoring to enhance the robustness of training framework. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world $4\times$ super-resolution benchmarks demonstrate that CLEAR consistently achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality and superior geometric fidelity.

</details>


### [240] [Self-supervised DXA representations encode multi-system disease risk, biological aging and heritability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02208)
*Gil Sasson,Zachary Levine,Smadar Shilo,Sarah Kohn,Guy Lutsker,Anastasia Godneva,Adam Gabet,David Krongauz,Adina Weinberger,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero,Eran Segal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LeDXA，一种基于JEPA架构的自我监督学习视觉模型，能从全身双能X射线吸收测定（DXA）扫描图像中提取系统性健康表征。仅用11,540张无标注图像训练，LeDXA在47,400张外部UK Biobank扫描上优于传统DXA测量和DINOv3（通用SOTA模型），显著提升疾病预测、生物年龄估计和遗传关联分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身DXA扫描常规用于测量骨密度和区域身体成分，但其蕴含的空间结构信息基本未被利用。作者希望证明自我监督学习能够将原始DXA图像转化为系统健康表征，挖掘常规读图中被丢弃的预后信息。

Method: 采用基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的视觉模型LeDXA，通过预测潜在表征而非重建像素来进行学习。在11,540张Human Phenotype Project无标注扫描上从头训练，并在47,400张外部UK Biobank扫描上评估。

Result: LeDXA在跨队列疾病和生物标志物预测上优于传统DXA测量和DINOv3（尽管训练图像少约15万倍、参数少约40倍）。极大改善了髋/膝骨关节炎和2型糖尿病的发病预测；外部年龄预测相关性r=0.88（MAE=2.90年）；生物年龄差距与疾病负担和45%更高死亡风险相关，且激素替代疗法可降低该差距；GWAS恢复已知位点且嵌入遗传度高于DINOv3。

Conclusion: DXA图像中包含常规测量丢弃的大量预后信息，且这些信息可用相对较少的数据和适度的计算量学习到，为系统性健康评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Whole-body dual-energy X-ray absorptiometry (DXA) scans are routinely acquired to measure bone density and regional body composition, leaving their spatial structure largely unused. Here, we show that self-supervised learning (SSL) can convert raw DXA images into representations of systemic health. We introduce LeDXA, a vision model based on a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) that learns by predicting latent representations rather than reconstructing pixels. Trained from scratch on 11,540 unlabeled Human Phenotype Project scans, LeDXA was evaluated internally and on 47,400 external UK Biobank (UKBB) scans. It improved cross-cohort prediction of prevalent diseases and biomarkers beyond scanner-derived DXA measurements and DINOv3, a state-of-the-art general-purpose model, despite approximately 150,000-fold fewer training images and nearly 40-fold fewer parameters. Over a median 4.3-year UKBB follow-up, LeDXA improved incident disease prediction over tabular DXA measures, with the largest gains for hip and knee arthrosis and type 2 diabetes. For hip arthrosis, 66% of incident cases occurred in the highest-risk quartile versus 41% for tabular measures. Its representations predicted chronological age externally (r = 0.88; mean absolute error = 2.90 years), and the biological-age gap tracked broader disease burden and a 45% higher mortality hazard in the oldest-appearing quartile. The gap also decreased in women after starting hormone-replacement therapy, suggesting it may be modifiable. Genome-wide associations recovered mostly known body-composition and bone-density loci, and LeDXA embeddings were more heritable than DINOv3's. These findings reveal prognostic information in DXA images that conventional readouts discard, learnable with relatively little data and modest compute.

</details>


### [241] [VARPose: Flexible 2D Pose Densification via Visual Autoregressive Modeling for Enhanced 3D Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02214)
*Kaiyuan Pu,Tiantian Yang,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VARPose将视觉自回归建模(VAR)从自然图像生成推广到人体骨骼这类拓扑结构化数据，通过自适应稠密化2D稀疏姿态来丰富3D提升模型可用的解剖信息。核心包括Granularity-agnostic Pose Tokenizer (GPT)和统一自回归模型UniSkelar，将"关节密度"视为"尺度"，以从粗到细的方式预测下一密度级别的token序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉自回归建模(VAR)在自然图像生成中表现优异，但尚未被应用于人体骨骼等拓扑结构化数据。稀疏的人体姿态数据缺乏足够的解剖信息，限制了后续3D姿态估计等任务的性能。

Method: 提出两个核心模块：1) Granularity-agnostic Pose Tokenizer (GPT)，采用单一混合码本和残差量化策略，将不同密度的姿态编码为统一的、多尺度的离散表示；2) UniSkelar，一个统一的自回归模型，将"关节密度"视为"尺度"，以从粗到细的方式预测下一密度级别的token序列，并以最稀疏姿态为条件。

Result: VARPose在性能上超越了最先进的方法，能够泛化到未见过的粒度，并通过2D姿态稠密化为3D姿态估计和人体网格恢复等下游任务带来了实际的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将表示与投影解耦，可以使用冻结的码本和重训练的解码器成功解码新的姿态粒度，表明该离散表示具有很强的泛化能力。代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Visual AutoRegressive Modeling (VAR) has excelled in natural image generation via next-scale prediction, but its use on topology-structured data like human skeletons is still unexplored. VARPose is proposed to adaptively densify 2D sparse poses, thereby enriching the anatomical information available for 3D lifting models. Our core contributions are twofold. First, we introduce a Granularity-agnostic Pose Tokenizer (GPT), which employs a single hybrid codebook and a residual quantization strategy to encode poses of varying densities into a unified, multi-scale discrete representation. Our results demonstrate the strong generalizability of this representation. By decoupling the representation from the projection, we can successfully decode novel pose granularities using a frozen codebook with a retrained decoder. Second, we propose UniSkelar, a unified autoregressive model that treats "joint density" as "scale". UniSkelar learns to predict the token sequence for the next density level in a coarse-to-fine manner, conditioned on the sparsest pose. VARPose not only outperforms state-of-the-art methods and generalizes to unseen granularities, but also confers tangible performance gains on downstream tasks, such as 3D Pose Estimation and Human Mesh Recovery, through 2D pose densification. Our code and model are available at https://github.com/BRL-SYSU/VARPose.git.

</details>


### [242] [Local Margin Restoration for Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02216)
*Yan Huang,Guowei Wang,Xu Wang,Kangjun Liu,Xin Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Local Margin Restoration (LMR)，一种轻量级单步测试时自适应(TTA)框架，用于缓解CLIP等视觉语言模型在分布偏移下的性能退化。LMR包含样本级的Protected Margin Restoration (PMR)目标函数和流级双阶段稳定机制（Adaptive Margin控制器与Bias Correction），有效防止梯度累积导致的模式崩溃，在CIFAR-C、ImageNet-C等基准上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉语言模型在零样本场景下性能出色，但在测试时遭遇分布偏移时性能会急剧下降。虽然TTA是可行方案，但连续在未标注测试流上自适应存在两个根本问题：1) 传统top-1主导的更新会通过破坏相关类别间的局部语义几何结构而加剧错误；2) 迭代自适应会累积渐进偏差，最终导致模型模式崩溃。

Method: 提出LMR框架，包含两个核心设计：1) 样本级——Protected Margin Restoration (PMR)目标，通过保护接近top的候选类别免受外部硬负样本干扰，恢复局部语义几何结构；2) 流级——双阶段稳定机制，包括Adaptive Margin (AM)控制器和Bias Correction (BC)，动态破坏渐进偏差累积并防止模式崩溃。整个框架为轻量级、单步TTA。

Result: 在CIFAR-C、ImageNet-C和ImageNet变体数据集上的大量实验表明，LMR持续超越现有SOTA TTA基线方法，且在具有挑战性的低batch测试时场景下表现出卓越的鲁棒性和效率。

Conclusion: LMR通过同时解决样本级语义几何破坏和流级偏差累积两个耦合问题，实现了鲁棒且高效的测试时自适应，为VLM在动态分布偏移场景下的可靠部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP exhibit remarkable zero-shot capabilities, yet their performance frequently degrades sharply under unexpected test-time distribution shifts. While Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) offers a promising solution, continuously adapting VLMs over an unlabeled test stream presents fundamental challenges. Conventional top-1-centric updates often reinforce errors by corrupting the local semantic geometry among related classes, while iterative adaptation exacerbates progressive bias accumulation, ultimately driving the model toward mode collapse. To overcome these coupled vulnerabilities, we propose Local Margin Restoration (LMR), a lightweight, one-step TTA framework. At the sample level, our Protected Margin Restoration (PMR) objective recovers local semantic geometry by shielding plausible near-top candidates from external hard negatives. Concurrently, to combat stream-level degradation, we introduce a dual-stage stabilization mechanism, featuring an Adaptive Margin (AM) controller and Bias Correction (BC), to dynamically disrupt progressive bias accumulation and prevent mode collapse. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-C, ImageNet-C, and ImageNet variants demonstrate that LMR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art TTA baselines, proving exceptionally robust and efficient even in challenging low-batch test-time regimes. Our code is available at https://github.com/DennisHuangYan/LMR.

</details>


### [243] [VC-Tooler: Learning Compositional and Adaptive Visual Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02217)
*Yizheng Wu,Jiashen Hua,Bing Deng,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VC-Tooler是一个将视觉工具使用学习为组合性和适应性能力的框架，通过分层合成流水线构建轨迹库，并采用监督冷启动加强化学习的两阶段训练，在通用和智能体基准上达到开源模型最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体多模态推理方法主要聚焦于固定工具空间和僵化调用模式中的接地（grounding），未充分解决工具组合（composition）和对工具返回观测的适应性推理（adaptation）这两个关键能力。

Method: 首先通过分层合成流水线构建轨迹库（trajectory bank），涵盖三种能力层级：单工具接地、多工具组合、多样工具上下文与接口；随后采用两阶段训练：监督冷启动建立基础能力，再通过强化学习鼓励准确、高效且上下文感知的视觉工具使用。

Result: VC-Tooler在通用和智能体基准上达到开源模型最优性能，包括V*上95.8%和VTC-Bench上35.3%的准确率，并在推理时更丰富的工具设置下展现出有前景的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉工具使用建模为组合性和适应性能力并结合两阶段训练策略，VC-Tooler有效弥补了现有方法在工具组合和适应性推理上的不足，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic multimodal reasoning extends passive image understanding by allowing VLMs to actively acquire and refine visual evidence through visual tool interactions. Effective visual tool use requires three capabilities: grounding tool calls in visual context, composing tools across multiple steps, and adapting reasoning to tool-returned observations. However, existing approaches largely focus on grounding within fixed tool spaces and rigid invocation patterns, leaving composition and adaptation insufficiently addressed. We present VC-Tooler, which learns visual tool use as a compositional and adaptive capability. To this end, we first build a trajectory bank through a hierarchical synthesis pipeline covering three capability levels: single-tool grounding, multi-tool composition, and diverse tool contexts and interfaces. We then train the model in two stages: a supervised cold start that establishes these capabilities, followed by reinforcement learning that encourages accurate, efficient, and context-aware visual tool use. VC-Tooler achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models on both general-purpose and agentic benchmarks, including $95.8\%$ on V* and $35.3\%$ on VTC-Bench, and shows promising transfer under richer tool settings at inference time. Project page: https://w1zheng.github.io/VC-Tooler

</details>


### [244] [GenPrior: Unleashing Text-to-Motion Generative Priors for Zero-Shot Skeleton-based Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02236)
*Jidong Kuang,Hongsong Wang,Jie Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GenPrior框架，首次利用预训练Text-to-Motion (T2M)模型的生成先验来解决零样本骨架动作识别（ZSAR）中的"语义-运动学鸿沟"问题。通过Dispersion-Gated Feature Fusion将运动学原型和类内离散度蒸馏到文本嵌入中，并利用Generative Prototype Refinement校准类原型，在NTU-60、NTU-120和PKU-MMD上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZSAR方法依赖文本派生原型，这些原型天生缺乏几何结构和物理约束，导致显著的语义-运动学鸿沟（semantic-kinematic gap），限制了识别性能。

Method: 1) Dispersion-Gated Feature Fusion：从T2M生成的运动序列中蒸馏运动学原型和类内离散度，通过学习的门控网络自适应地将可靠的结构线索注入文本嵌入，同时抑制合成伪影。2) Generative Prototype Refinement：利用生成增强原型作为锚点挖掘高置信度的未见类样本，将类原型向真实分布校准。

Result: 在NTU-60、NTU-120和PKU-MMD三个基准数据集上，GenPrior在零样本和广义零样本设置下均达到最优性能。

Conclusion: 利用T2M生成模型的先验知识可以有效弥补文本语义与骨架运动学之间的鸿沟，为ZSAR提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Zero-shot skeleton-based action recognition (ZSAR) aims to recognize unseen action categories by aligning skeleton features with textual semantics. However, existing methods rely on text-derived prototypes that inherently lack geometric structure and physical constraints, resulting in a pronounced \textit{semantic-kinematic gap}. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{GenPrior}, the first framework to exploit generative priors from pre-trained Text-to-Motion (T2M) models for ZSAR. Specifically, we introduce Dispersion-Gated Feature Fusion, which distills kinematic prototypes and intra-class dispersion from generative motion sequences and employs a learned gating network to adaptively inject reliable structural cues into textual embeddings while suppressing synthetic artifacts. Furthermore, we propose Generative Prototype Refinement, which leverages these generation-enhanced prototypes as anchors to mine high-confidence unseen samples, calibrating class prototypes toward the true distribution and thereby unleashing strong performance gains. Extensive experiments on NTU-60, NTU-120, and PKU-MMD demonstrate that GenPrior achieves state-of-the-art performance under both zero-shot and generalized zero-shot settings. Code is available at https://github.com/jidongkuang/GenPrior.

</details>


### [245] [HarMoE: Multi-Source Chest Radiograph Pretraining with Dataset-Disentangled Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02252)
*Haozhe Luo,Ziyu Zhou,Shelley Zixin Shu,Mauricio Reyes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HarMoE是一个面向胸部X光影像的视觉-语言模型框架，通过数据集感知的混合专家（mixture-of-experts）机制，从多个异构多标签分类数据集中学习统一的临床语义，同时将数据集特有的变化隔离到浅层残差专家中，从而在零样本分类、分布外泛化和接地任务上优于现有强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有胸部X光视觉语言模型主要依赖MIMIC-CXR进行图像-报告对齐预训练，忽略了多标签分类数据集这一更干净、更明确且覆盖更广的监督来源。然而异构数据集在标签本体、标注协议、采集流程上的差异会导致模型将临床语义与数据集身份纠缠，损害迁移性能。

Method: 提出HarMoE框架：(1) 数据集感知的混合专家架构，共享跨数据集医学语义并将源特有变化限制在深层解码器的轻量残差专家中；(2) 统一疾病词汇表下的掩码多数据集监督训练，利用互补标注且避免假阴性问题。

Result: 在大规模胸部X光基准上，HarMoE在零样本分类、分布外迁移和接地任务上持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: 构建鲁棒的放射学视觉语言模型不应局限于单一来源的图像-报告对齐，而应从异构数据集出发进行结构化知识构建，利用更干净的监督信号和更广泛的病理覆盖。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models for chest X-ray understanding are largely built on image-report alignment and therefore rely heavily on MIMIC-CXR as the dominant pretraining source. While effective at scale, this paradigm underexplores an important alternative source of supervision: a range of existing multi-label classification datasets, which provide cleaner and more explicit disease signals than free-text reports, and can offer broader pathology coverage when combined across sources. However, learning from such heterogeneous datasets is nontrivial, as differences in label ontologies, annotation protocols, acquisition pipelines, and report styles can cause models to entangle clinical semantics with dataset identity, leading to poor transfer despite increased scale. In this work, we revisit radiology VLM construction from the perspective of harmonized multi-source learning. We propose HarMoE, a dataset-aware mixture-of-experts framework that learns shared cross-dataset medical semantics while confining source-specific variation to lightweight residual experts in deeper decoder layers. To further exploit clean supervision from labeled datasets, we train in a unified disease vocabulary with masked multi-dataset supervision, enabling the model to leverage complementary annotations without introducing false negatives. Experiments on large-scale chest X-ray benchmarks show that HarMoE consistently improves zero-shot classification, out-of-distribution transfer, and grounding over strong baselines. Our results suggest that building robust radiology VLMs requires moving beyond single-source image-report alignment toward structured knowledge construction from heterogeneous datasets with cleaner supervision and broader coverage. Code and the 873k harmonized dataset will be released at https://github.com/Roypic/harmoe.

</details>


### [246] [Open-Set Visual Text Forensics via Sparse-Constraint Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02258)
*Jiangling Zhang,Shuxuan Gao,Zeyu Chen,Yichao Liu,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种生成式文本篡改检测器SC-RF，通过估计局部修复代价来定位被篡改的视觉文本区域，而非学习伪造特定的决策边界。该方法在三个基准上达到SOTA，F1和IoU分别超越第二名3.2和4.8个百分点，并在未见过的文本编辑模式上展现出强大的零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有判别式取证模型往往过拟合特定伪造模式，难以泛化到未见过的开放集攻击。随着生成式AI快速演进，视觉文本操纵日益复杂，亟需一种不依赖伪造特定决策边界的检测方法，以应对不断涌现的新攻击类型。

Method: 1) 提出生成式检测框架，通过估计将查询图像与真实视觉文本统计对齐所需的局部修复代价来定位篡改区域；2) 引入稀疏约束整流流(SC-RF)，这是Flow Matching的检测器导向适配，用于空间稀疏异常定位；3) 通过自监督伪造注入(Artifact Injection)缓解数据稀缺问题；4) 使用像素空间的Forensic-DiT保留高频取证痕迹。

Result: 在三个基准上达到SOTA性能，F1和IoU分别超越第二名3.2和4.8个百分点。在未见过的文本编辑模式上展现强大的零样本检测能力。此外，应力测试分析表明模型产生的局部协调可以削弱现有检测器依赖的统计线索，提供补充性漏洞分析视角。

Conclusion: 生成式检测范式通过估计修复代价而非学习伪造特定边界，能有效应对开放集文本篡改攻击，具有更好的泛化能力。同时该方法也揭示了现有检测器的脆弱性，为取证领域提供了双向价值。

Abstract: Rapidly evolving Generative AI enables sophisticated visual text manipulations that increasingly evade current forensic detectors. Existing discriminative models often overfit specific forgery patterns, limiting their generalization to unseen, open-set attacks. To address this challenge, we propose a generative detector that localizes tampering by estimating the local restoration cost required to align a query image with authentic visual-text statistics, rather than by learning forgery-specific decision boundaries. Specifically, we introduce Sparse-Constraint Rectified Flow (SC-RF), a detector-oriented adaptation of Flow Matching for spatially sparse anomaly localization. We further mitigate data scarcity via self-supervised Artifact Injection and preserve high-frequency forensic traces using a pixel-space Forensic-DiT. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing the runner-up by 3.2 and 4.8 percentage points in F1 and IoU, respectively. In particular, the proposed detector demonstrates strong zero-shot performance on challenging unseen text editing patterns. We further provide an auxiliary stress-test analysis showing that local harmonization produced by our model can weaken the statistical cues relied upon by existing detectors, offering a complementary vulnerability-analysis perspective.

</details>


### [247] [EOVSAM: Efficient Open-Vocabulary Segmentation with SAM 3 in One Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02284)
*Haomin Peng,Yongkang Li,Zhaoxiang Liu,Xiaojie Jin,Shiguo Lian,Yunchao Wei,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EOVSAM框架，将SAM 3从耗时的穷举式词汇遍历改为单次前向预测，去除提示条件将其变为高效掩码生成器，并引入注意力聚合策略进行端到端的开放词汇分类优化，在提升分割精度的同时将推理速度提升高达338倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 3虽然支持名词短语引导的分割并取得了有竞争力的开放词汇性能，但其通过穷举式词汇遍历实现，导致在目标类别规模增大时计算开销巨大，难以实际部署。现有方法依赖多阶段流程和后处理启发式，亟需一种高效且准确的开放词汇分割方案。

Method: EOVSAM包含两个关键设计：(1) 去除SAM 3的提示条件化机制，将其改造为高效的单次前向掩码生成器；(2) 提出注意力聚合（Attentional Aggregation）策略，用于端到端优化开放词汇分类。该公式避免了多阶段流程和后处理启发式，同时缓解了直接优化分类时可能出现的闭集坍缩（closed-set collapse）问题。

Result: EOVSAM在所有评估数据集上一致优于vanilla SAM 3的分割精度，推理速度提升高达338倍。在较低分辨率下仍保持高精度，同时获得更显著的推理速度优势。在标准语义和全景分割基准上达到竞争性或最先进的精度，同时具备大幅速度优势。

Conclusion: EOVSAM成功将SAM 3从低效的穷举遍历范式转变为高效的单次前向分割框架，在保持乃至提升精度的同时实现了数量级的推理加速，为开放词汇分割提供了兼顾精度与速度的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary segmentation identifies and segments objects from arbitrary textual descriptions. SAM 3 supports noun-phrase-guided segmentation and achieves competitive open-vocabulary performance through exhaustive vocabulary traversal, yet suffers from prohibitive computational overhead as target categories scale. In this paper, we propose an Efficient Open-Vocabulary segmentation framework with SAM 3 (EOVSAM), which adapts SAM 3 for single-pass prediction. EOVSAM removes prompt conditioning to turn SAM 3 into an efficient mask generator and introduces a new Attentional Aggregation strategy to optimize open-vocabulary classification end-to-end. This formulation avoids the multi-stage pipelines and post-processing heuristics commonly used by existing methods, while mitigating the closed-set collapse that can arise when classification is optimized directly. EOVSAM consistently improves segmentation accuracy over vanilla SAM 3 on all evaluated datasets and accelerates inference by up to 338$\times$. Furthermore, EOVSAM maintains high accuracy at lower resolutions while achieving even more remarkable inference speeds. Experiments on standard semantic and panoptic segmentation benchmarks show that EOVSAM combines competitive or state-of-the-art accuracy with a substantial speed advantage over existing open-vocabulary segmentation models. Code and models are available at https://github.com/hustvl/EOVSAM.

</details>


### [248] [Sen-Cap: Sensor-Flexible and Noise-Resilient Human Motion Capture via LiDAR-Camera Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02285)
*Aoru Xue,Yujing Sun,Yiming Ren,Kwok-Yan Lam,Mao Ye,Yuexin Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sen-Cap是一个传感器灵活且抗噪声的3D人体运动捕捉框架，融合LiDAR和相机多模态数据，无需传感器间标定即可重构人体姿态与形状，并通过迭代精化在严重点云噪声下保持鲁棒性，在多个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态人体运动捕捉方法面临两大核心挑战：(1) 跨传感器对齐/匹配依赖显式标定，在视角变化下传播误差并限制部署为固定高度重叠布局；(2) 在真实环境中常见的严重噪声或部分传感器故障下性能大幅退化。

Method: Sen-Cap包含两个关键组件：一是统一跨传感器运动估计器（Unified Across-Sensor Motion Estimator），在人体中心空间中重构局部姿态与形状，无需传感器间标定，支持任意数量的传感器；二是抗噪声轨迹追踪器（Noise-Resistant Trajectory Tracker），通过迭代精化在严重点云噪声下保持鲁棒性。

Result: Sen-Cap实时运行，在Human-M3和FreeMotion数据集上的主要指标达到最先进性能，并在LiDARHuman26M和RELI11D上展现出强大的跨域表现。

Conclusion: Sen-Cap将传感器灵活性与噪声鲁棒性结合，为体育分析、野外机器人和大规模沉浸式环境等真实世界场景中的运动捕捉开辟了新机遇，使系统部署更具实用性。

Abstract: We propose Sen-Cap, a Sensor-Flexible and Noise-Resilient 3D human motion Capture framework that integrates multi-modal data from LiDAR and camera. While multi-modal sensors provide richer information than single-modal sensors, existing approaches still suffer from two core challenges. First, multi-modal alignment/matching across arbitrarily deployed sensors is typically handled by explicit calibration, which propagates errors under changing viewpoints and in turn constrains deployment to fixed, highly overlapped layouts. Second, prior methods degrade under severe noise or partial sensor failures, which are common in real-world environments. To address these challenges, Sen-Cap introduces a Unified Across-Sensor Motion Estimator that reconstructs local pose and shape in a human-centric space without calibrations between sensors, supporting a flexible number of sensors, as well as a Noise-Resistant Trajectory Tracker that maintains robustness under severe point cloud noise through iterative refinement. These sensor-flexible and noise-resilient features make Sen-Cap more practical in real-world deployment. Notably, operating in real time, Sen-Cap achieves state-of-the-art performance on major metrics on Human-M3 and FreeMotion, as well as strong cross-domain performance on LiDARHuman26M and RELI11D. This combination of flexibility and robustness opens new opportunities for motion capture in real-world scenarios, e.g. sports analytics, field robotics, and large-scale immersive environments.

</details>


### [249] [Extended Field of View Analysis for VideoGAN-based Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02289)
*Annajoyce Mariani,Kira Maag,Hanno Gottschalk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在以往基于GAN的语义鸟瞰图交通场景生成工作的基础上，扩展了生成框架：改进了语义表示、用图关联方法替代轨迹提取、系统研究更大视野范围，并引入定量评估框架用于检测生成视频中的幻觉和物体持久性问题。实验表明该框架能泛化到更大更复杂的交通场景，在150 GPU小时的训练和低于20ms的推理时间内，可生成长达20秒的场景，证明视频GAN仍是高效、可扩展的真实轨迹生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现更高级别的车辆自动化需要真实且多样的轨迹生成，但规则方法和经典学习方法难以捕捉交通行为的复杂性，因此需要借助在复杂建模方面表现出色的生成模型来生成高质量交通轨迹。

Method: 1) 改进语义鸟瞰图表示；2) 用基于图的关联方法替代原有的轨迹提取过程；3) 系统测试不同大小的视野范围；4) 提出定量评估框架来衡量生成视频中的幻觉现象和物体持久性。

Result: 框架能够泛化到更大更复杂的交通场景，生成统计上真实且空间关系连贯的轨迹；仅需150 GPU小时训练，推理时间低于20ms即可生成长达20秒的场景。

Conclusion: 视频生成对抗网络仍然是高效且可扩展的轨迹生成方法，即使在更大的交通场景中也能保持良好性能，适用于自动驾驶中的预测、规划和仿真等下游任务。

Abstract: Realistic and diverse trajectory generation is central to enabling higher levels of vehicle automation. While rule-based and classical learning-based methods may struggle to capture the complexity of traffic behavior, generative models have already demonstrated in other fields that they can handle a comparable level of complexity. In this paper, we build upon previous work on generative adversarial network (GAN)-based semantic bird's-eye-view traffic generation and extend the proposed framework in several key aspects. We improve the semantic representation, replace the trajectory extraction procedure with a graph-based association method, and systematically investigate increasingly larger fields of view. In addition, we introduce a quantitative evaluation framework to assess hallucinations and object permanence in generated videos. Our experiments demonstrate that the framework generalizes to larger and more complex traffic scenes while maintaining statistically realistic trajectories and coherent spatial relationships between traffic participants. Within 150GPU hours of training and with inference times below 20ms for scenes of up to 20s, our results demonstrate that video-based GANs remain an efficient and scalable approach for realistic trajectory generation, even in substantially larger traffic scenes, making them well suited for downstream tasks such as prediction, planning, and simulation in automated driving.

</details>


### [250] [SpikeRestormer: Towards Energy-Efficient All-in-One Image Restoration via Unified Event Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02290)
*Shengkai Hu,Jie Shao,Jiaqi Ma,Xu Zhang,Keying Wu,Qilu Zhu,Beihang Song,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpikeRestormer，一种基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的全能图像修复方法。通过内部生成脉冲事件并进行事件推理，解决了SNN应用于静态图像时的挑战，在保持极低能耗的同时实现了与ANN方法竞争的性能，并在SNN方法中达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于ANN的全能图像修复（AiOIR）虽然能处理多种退化，但计算成本高，难以实时部署。SNN虽然低功耗，但应用于静态图像时缺乏显式事件信号，且退化线索与场景结构纠缠在一起，难以学习可靠的修复导向脉冲事件。

Method: 提出SpikeRestormer框架，包含三个互补过程：(1) 退化事件感知：通过Subtractive Degradation Event Attention (SDEA)提取基于脉冲的退化事件；(2) 事件可靠性推断：通过Hierarchical Bayesian Skip Masking (HBSM)进行；(3) 修复事件构建：通过Additive Restoration Event Attention (AREA)完成。三者整合形成统一的修复流程。

Result: 大量实验表明，SpikeRestormer在与ANN方法竞争时表现相当，并在SNN方法中建立了新的最先进结果，同时显著降低了能耗。

Conclusion: SpikeRestormer通过将修复任务公式化为退化事件感知、退化事件可靠性推断和修复事件构建的统一过程，释放了SNN在低能耗全能图像修复中的潜力。

Abstract: ANN-based All-in-One image restoration (AiOIR) unifies diverse degradation handling but incurs high computational costs, limiting its real-time deployment. While Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer a low-power alternative, applying them to static images remains challenging. This difficulty arises because explicit event signals are absent, and degradation cues are heavily entangled with scene structures, hindering the learning of reliable restoration-oriented spike events. To address these issues, we propose SpikeRestormer, an energy-efficient SNN for AiOIR that performs event reasoning over internally generated spike cues. Specifically, we propose a degradation-event perception process to extract spike-based degradation events through Subtractive Degradation Event Attention (SDEA). Moreover, we introduce Hierarchical Bayesian Skip Masking (HBSM) and Additive Restoration Event Attention (AREA) processes for event-reliability inference and restoration-event construction, respectively. By integrating these complementary processes, SpikeRestormer formulates restoration as a unified process of degradation-event perception, degradation-event reliability inference, and restoration-event construction, liberating the potential of SNNs for energy-efficient AiOIR. Extensive experiments show that SpikeRestormer delivers competitive performance against ANN-based methods and establishes new state-of-the-art results among SNN-based methods with significantly lower energy consumption.

</details>


### [251] [A General-Purpose VLM Can Teach an Astronomy Foundation Model to Better Recognize Galaxy Morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02300)
*Dichang Zhang,Jiaqi Deng,Yixuan Shao,Yuanpeng Liu,Jiali Cui,Zhiqiang Lao,Heather Yu,Liang Peng,Simon Birrer,Dimitris Samaras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用通用视觉语言模型（VLM）作为弱监督信号，为天文基础模型Zoobot提供形态学知识，在有限人工标注预算下显著提升星系形态分类性能，并构建了面向巡天任务的VQA基准来评估VLM的形态学能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的天文学基础模型虽然能提供强大的星系表征，但将其适配到新的巡天条件和特定形态学识别任务时，仍需要大量人工监督。为了在有限人工标注预算下高效利用这些模型，作者探索VLM中蕴含的视觉-语义先验知识能否作为弱监督信号，提升下游形态学分类性能。

Method: 1) 构建了面向巡天的VQA基准，覆盖两个代表性成像体制，评估多种最先进VLM在星系形态学问题上的表现。2) 研究发现VLM虽然不够可靠以完全替代人工标注，但能捕获有用的形态学信号和不确定性信息。3) 基于此发现，使用通用VLM作为"形态学教师"来指导Zoobot（基于大规模Galaxy Zoo标注预训练的天文基础模型），在两个巡天域和多种标注预算下进行知识蒸馏。

Result: 实验表明，在多个标注预算和两个巡天领域下，VLM教师持续改善了Zoobot的下游形态学分类性能，证明通用VLM提供了与天文学基础模型互补的知识，能在有限人工监督下教会其更好地识别星系形态。

Conclusion: VLM与天文学基础模型的结合为标签高效的巡天适配提供了可行路径，特别适用于即将到来的大型巡天项目如Vera C. Rubin天文台的LSST和Nancy Grace Roman太空望远镜。代码和基准已开源。

Abstract: Existing astronomy foundation models provide strong galaxy representations, but adapting them to new survey conditions and survey-specific morphology recognition tasks still requires substantial human supervision. We show that VLM-based VQA systems contain meaningful visual-semantic priors that can serve as weak supervision for downstream morphology classifiers and improve morphology classification under limited human-label budgets. We first introduce a survey-oriented VQA benchmark spanning two representative imaging regimes and evaluate state-of-the-art VLMs on galaxy morphology questions. The results show that these models capture useful morphology signals and informative uncertainty, but are not sufficiently reliable to replace human annotators. Motivated by this finding, we use a general-purpose VLM as a morphology teacher for Zoobot, an astronomy foundation model pretrained on large-scale Galaxy Zoo annotations. Across two survey domains and multiple annotation budgets, the VLM teacher consistently improves Zoobot's downstream morphology classification. These results demonstrate that a general-purpose VLM provides knowledge complementary to an astronomy foundation model and can teach it to better recognize galaxy morphology under limited human supervision. The resulting pipeline is designed for label-efficient adaptation to forthcoming large-scale surveys, including the Vera C. Rubin Observatory's Legacy Survey of Space and Time (LSST) and the Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope. The benchmark and code are publicly available at https://github.com/fw-ic/VLM-morphology-teacher.

</details>


### [252] [The Push-Forward Transform for Continuous and Robust Comparison of Dynamic Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02306)
*Roua Rouatbi,Juan-Esteban Suarez Cardona,Ivo F. Sbalzarini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Push-Forward Transform（PF-T）的数学框架，通过将形状域映射到共同参考域来实现形状比较。该变换应用于Signed Distance Functions（SDFs），能产生捕捉边界和内部几何的连续表示，支持2D、3D及时间演化几何体的不变且鲁棒的形状比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形状比较及其时间演化分析是图像分析中的基础挑战。有意义的形状比较需要表示对平移、旋转、反射、重参数化和均匀缩放等不影响形状本身的变换保持不变，同时对内在几何变化保持敏感。现有方法依赖于敏感的参数化、地标对应或难以解释和复现的学习表示。

Method: 将Push-Forward Transform应用于Signed Distance Functions，生成连续的形状表示。该表示能同时捕捉边界和内部几何信息，并推导出可解释的形态计量学指标，用于量化形状相似性并揭示骨骼拓扑和旋转对称性等特征。该方法统一适用于2D和3D形状，可扩展到时间演化几何体，并支持形状上附加标量场（如强度或分子信号）的联合分析。

Result: 论文提供了数学公式、高效的算法实现，并在2D、3D和时间序列数据集上进行了基准测试，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PF-T提供了一种可解释、可复现且具有不变性的形状比较框架，克服了现有方法在参数化敏感性、可解释性和跨维度适用性方面的局限。

Abstract: We introduce a mathematical framework for shape comparison based on mapping functions from the shape domain to a common reference domain. This Push-Forward Transform enables invariant and robust comparison of shapes, preserving intrinsic geometric information. Quantitatively comparing shapes and their temporal evolution is a fundamental challenge in image analysis. Meaningful shape comparison requires representations that are invariant to transformations that do not alter shape itself, such as translation, rotation, reflection, re-parametrization, and uniform scaling, while remaining sensitive to intrinsic geometric variation. Existing approaches often rely on sensitive parameterizations, landmark correspondence, or learned representations that are difficult to interpret and reproduce. We show that the Push-Forward Transform (PF-T) applied to Signed Distance Functions (SDFs) yields a continuous representation that captures both boundary and interior geometry. We derive an interpretable morphometric that quantifies shape similarity and reveals features such as skeletal topology and rotational symmetries. The push-forward transform applies consistently to two- and three-dimensional shapes, extends to time-evolving geometries, and supports the joint analysis of shape and additional scalar fields defined over shapes, such as intensity or molecular signals. We present the mathematical formulation, describe an efficient algorithm, and benchmark the approach on 2D, 3D, and temporal data sets.

</details>


### [253] [CalibBEV: LiDAR-Camera Calibration via BEV Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02309)
*Filippo D'Addeo,Lorenzo Cipelli,Adriano Cardace,Emanuele Ghelfi,Andrea Zinelli,Massimo Bertozzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CalibBEV是一种新颖的基于鸟瞰图(BEV)的LiDAR-相机标定方法，通过将两种传感器数据统一到共享的3D空间表示中，采用两步对齐流程（隐式粗标定+显式精标定）实现高精度的跨模态标定，在KITTI和nuScenes基准上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR-相机标定方法主要依赖逐点对应或点-像素匹配，这些方法在特征稀疏、外观差异大的跨模态场景中鲁棒性不足，难以实现高精度标定。作者希望通过统一的BEV空间表示来克服跨模态特征差异问题。

Method: 1) 使用领域特定的架构分别从LiDAR和相机数据提取BEV特征；2) 第一步通过隐式对齐直接从BEV特征回归粗标定矩阵，并利用类似CLIP的对比损失强制两个模态的BEV表示在语义上保持一致；3) 第二步通过显式地将一个模态的特征对齐到另一个模态，细化粗标定结果得到最终精确的标定矩阵。

Result: 在KITTI和nuScenes基准上，相比之前的方法，RRE分别降低51%和68%，RTE分别降低80%和91%，达到了当前最优的标定精度。

Conclusion: CalibBEV通过统一的BEV空间表示和两步对齐策略，有效解决了跨模态标定中的特征匹配难题，在精度和鲁棒性上均实现了显著的性能提升，验证了BEV表示在传感器标定任务中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We present CalibBEV, a novel Bird's Eye View (BEV) alignment approach for LiDAR-camera calibration. Our method unifies LiDAR and camera data into a shared 3D spatial representation, enabling accurate and robust cross-modal calibration. CalibBEV extracts sensor-wise BEV features from each modality using domain-specific architectures and estimates the calibration matrix through a two-step alignment process. First, we perform an implicit alignment by regressing a coarse calibration matrix directly from the BEV features. To ease this alignment, we enforce semantic consistency between BEV representations across modalities using a contrastive loss inspired by CLIP, guiding both networks toward a unified feature space. In the second step, we leverage our BEV formulation to explicitly align the features of one modality with the other, refining the initial coarse estimate into a final, more accurate calibration matrix. CalibBEV significantly outperforms prior point-to-pixel matching methods, achieving state-of-the-art calibration accuracy. On the KITTI and nuScenes benchmarks, our method reduces the Relative Rotation Error (RRE) by 51% and 68%, and the Relative Translation Error (RTE) by 80% and 91%, respectively, compared to previous methods.

</details>


### [254] [GEOID-Flood: A Large-Scale Multi-Modal Benchmark Dataset for Flood Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02315)
*Gaetano Chiriaco,Luca Barco,Andrea Bragagnolo,Claudio Rossi,Edoardo Arnaudo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GEOID-Flood，一个大规模多模态洪水分割基准数据集，包含65个国家219个事件、超过14,000个瓦片，提供双时相SAR（GRD和RTC格式）、Sentinel-2光学影像、DEM以及人工验证标签。通过评估基础模型与传统编码器在单图像、多时相和多模态协议下的表现，发现基础模型有适度优势，光学-SAR融合微调最能解决瞬时洪水，且在该数据集上训练的模型泛化能力更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的洪水测绘数据集很少在大规模尺度上结合双时相SAR和配准的光学影像，导致地理空间基础模型在此下游任务中的价值缺乏系统评估，亟需一个大规模、高质量、多模态的基准数据集来检验基础模型的表征迁移能力。

Method: 从哥白尼应急管理服务数据中构建GEOID-Flood基准，提供超过14,000个瓦片，涵盖219个事件、65个国家、十年跨度。数据包括事件前后的Sentinel-1（GRD和RTC格式）、事件前Sentinel-2合成影像、DEM，以及区分背景、永久水和洪水的标注。在此基础上对比基础模型与传统编码器在单图像、多时相和多模态三种协议下的性能。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）基础模型相比传统编码器提供一致但适度的性能优势；（2）光学-SAR融合并结合微调最能有效解决瞬时洪水识别；（3）在GEOID-Flood上训练的模型对未见事件的迁移能力优于在现有数据集上训练的模型。

Conclusion: GEOID-Flood作为大规模多模态洪水分割基准，填补了该领域数据空白，证明了基础模型在洪水测绘任务中的价值，并展示了多模态融合和领域内训练对模型泛化能力的积极影响。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models aim to learn representations that transfer across regions and sensors, yet evaluating them on specific tasks requires large, high-quality, multi-modal benchmarks that measure how well such models extract value from data. Concerning flood mapping, existing datasets rarely combine bi-temporal SAR and co-registered optical imagery at scale, leaving the value of foundation models for this downstream task largely untested. We introduce GEOID-Flood, a large-scale multi-modal flood segmentation benchmark, derived from Copernicus Emergency Management Service activations, spanning 219 events across 65 countries over ten years. The dataset provides more than 14,000 tiles with co-registered pre- and post-event Sentinel-1, in GRD and RTC format, pre-event Sentinel-2 composite, and DEM, including manually validated labels that separate background from permanent water and flooded water. Using this benchmark, we evaluate foundation models against conventional encoders across single-image, multi-temporal, and multi-modal protocols. We report three main findings: foundation models offer a consistent but modest advantage; optical-SAR fusion with finetuning best resolves transient flooding; and models trained on GEOID-Flood transfer to unseen events better than those trained on existing datasets. Dataset and code available at https://github.com/links-ads/geoid-flood.

</details>


### [255] [Global-Scale Self-Supervised Spatiotemporal Learning for NDVI Time-Series Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02322)
*Ang Li,Menghui Jiang,Xiaobin Guan,Dong Chu,Huanfeng Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GloSSR，一个全球尺度的自监督时空框架用于NDVI时间序列重建，通过人工模拟真实云污染模式构建自监督训练对，并结合双向Transformer与ConvLSTM架构联合捕捉长期时间依赖和短期时空相关性，在MODIS和AVHRR数据上均展现出优越的重建性能和广泛适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云污染和噪声导致NDVI时间序列不完整，而深度学习方法受限于难以获得同一时空位置上的干净与退化配对数据，亟需一种无需真实配对标签的自监督重建方案。

Method: 1) 通过人工模拟真实云污染模式退化相对干净的NDVI观测，构建自监督训练对；2) 设计端到端时空学习网络，采用双向Transformer结合ConvLSTM架构捕捉长期时间依赖与短期时空相关性；3) 引入基于时间-通道注意力的重建模块增强有效特征；4) 设计时空先验约束以在优化中保留细尺度结构和长期物候趋势。

Result: 在MODIS NDVI人工退化像元重建实验中持续优于对比方法；真实观测时间序列分析表明能准确刻画植被动态和关键物候状态；长期植被趋势分析和向AVHRR数据的迁移实验验证了框架的可扩展性和大规模环境监测的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: GloSSR通过自监督学习策略和融合Transformer与ConvLSTM的混合架构，解决了NDVI重建中配对数据稀缺的瓶颈，实现了准确且可扩展的全球尺度植被动态重建，对大规模环境监测具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient reconstruction of cloud-contaminated and noise-corrupted NDVI time series remains a challenge in remote sensing. Deep learning provides a promising solution for modeling complex spatiotemporal dependencies; however, its application is often limited by the difficulty of obtaining paired clear-sky and degraded NDVI data for identical spatiotemporal locations. To address this issue, we propose GloSSR, a Global-scale Self-supervised Spatiotemporal framework for NDVI Reconstruction. The framework constructs supervisory signals by artificially degrading relatively clean NDVI observations with realistic cloud contamination patterns, producing self-supervised training pairs that closely mimic real-world degradation. It further introduces an end-to-end spatiotemporal learning network that jointly captures long-range temporal dependencies and short-term spatiotemporal correlation through a bidirectional Transformer with a ConvLSTM architecture. A temporal-channel attention-based reconstruction module is incorporated to enhance informative features, while a spatiotemporal prior constraint is designed to preserve both fine-scale structures and long-term phenological trends during optimization. Extensive evaluations on MODIS NDVI data demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework across both artificial and real-world scenarios. In artificial degraded-pixel reconstruction experiments, GloSSR consistently outperforms the comparison methods. Time-series analyses based on real observations further demonstrate that the proposed framework can accurately characterize vegetation dynamics and capture the key phenological states. Long-term vegetation trend analysis and the transferability analysis to AVHRR data validate the scalability of the framework and illustrate its broad applicability for large-scale environmental monitoring.

</details>


### [256] [Implicit Neural Representations for Multimodal Longitudinal Image Imputation and Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02324)
*Sina Wendrich,Lukas Förner,Zoe Reinke,Kartikay Tehlan,Ansgar Berlis,Michael Frühwald,Matthias Wagner,Thomas Wendler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种患者特定的条件隐式神经表示（INR）模型，用于建模多模态纵向MRI数据，将其表示为世界坐标、时间和模态条件的连续函数。通过随机模态丢弃训练处理数据缺失，利用连续坐标空间实现空间和时间插值，并基于自一致性推导置信度估计器，在儿科脑肿瘤患者数据上验证了优于线性插值的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床上纵向多参数MRI随访成像至关重要，但真实临床数据常存在序列缺失、采集协议异质性以及跨时间点的空间分辨率差异等问题，传统方法难以有效处理这些不完整、异构的数据。

Method: 1) 提出患者特定的条件隐式神经表示（INR），将纵向多模态MRI建模为世界坐标、时间和模态条件的连续函数；2) 采用随机模态丢弃策略训练以处理不完整数据；3) 利用连续坐标空间实现无需重采样到固定体素网格的空间和时间插值；4) 基于跨模态重建性能推导出自一致性的置信度估计器。

Result: 在儿科脑肿瘤患者的纵向MRI上，T1CE和FLAIR模态相比线性插值有统计学显著提升（p<0.05），T1CE的MS-SSIM均值达0.95±0.02；预测置信度与真实重建质量强相关（Pearson r最高达0.996）。

Conclusion: 该框架在异构临床环境中具有可靠的部署潜力，能够有效处理纵向多模态MRI中的缺失数据和协议异质性，并提供了可信的置信度估计。

Abstract: Longitudinal multiparametric MRI is central to follow-up imaging in oncology, yet real-world clinical data are characterised by missing sequences, heterogeneous acquisition protocols, and varying spatial resolutions across time points. We propose a patient-specific conditional implicit neural representation (INR) that models multimodal longitudinal MRI as a continuous function of world coordinates, time, and modality conditioning. The model is trained with stochastic modality dropout to handle incomplete data, and its continuous coordinate-space formulation enables both spatial and temporal interpolation without resampling to a fixed voxel grid. A self-consistency-based confidence estimator is derived from cross-modal reconstruction performance at inference time. We evaluate the framework on longitudinal MRI from paediatric brain tumour patients, demonstrating statistically significant improvements over linear interpolation for T1CE and FLAIR (p < 0.05), with mean MS-SSIM of 0.95 $\pm$ 0.02 for T1CE. Predicted confidence correlates strongly with true reconstruction quality (Pearson r up to 0.996), suggesting reliable deployment potential in heterogeneous clinical settings.

</details>


### [257] [Context-Aware Mixture of Domain Experts for Bodily Expression of Emotion in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02331)
*Mohammad Mahdi Dehshibi,David Masip*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CA-MoDE框架，通过场景和物体专家生成领域条件化的情绪分布先验，在分布层级而非特征层级调制身体专家的预测，并采用max-endorsement门控策略融合多域信号，在Body Language Database上取得SOTA成绩（ERS 0.3269），仅用静态图像即超越时序模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同身体姿态在不同上下文中可传达完全不同的情绪，但现有方法仅将场景和物体线索视为辅助特征增强，而非作为情绪合理性的结构化先验，导致上下文信息利用不充分。

Method: 1) 构建场景专家和物体专家，生成各自领域条件下情绪类别的软分布；2) 以分布层级（而非特征层级）将领域条件化的软预测作为结构化先验来调控身体专家的输出；3) 提出任务定制的max-endorsement门控策略，在每个情绪维度上选择最强的上下文信号，避免平均化造成的信号稀释。

Result: 在Body Language Database上取得0.3269的情绪识别分数，超越现有仅依赖静态图像的时序模型。

Conclusion: 显式建模结构化空间上下文可作为视频行为动态的互补判别代理，说明上下文先验建模对肢体情绪识别的重要性。

Abstract: The same body posture can convey entirely different emotions depending on its surrounding context, yet most methods for recognising bodily emotions treat scene and object cues as auxiliary feature augmentations rather than as structured priors over the plausibility of emotions. We introduce the Context-Aware Mixture of Domain Experts (CA-MoDE) for bodily emotion recognition. CA-MoDE incorporates dedicated scene and object experts to generate soft distributions over emotion categories conditioned on their respective domains. These domain-conditioned soft predictions serve as structured contextual priors that modulate the body expert's predictions at the distributional level rather than at the feature level. To fuse these multi-domain signals, we propose a task-tailored max-endorsement gating strategy that selects the strongest contextual signal across experts for each emotion dimension. Our gating strategy mitigates the signal dilution that typically occurs when conflicting or uninformative context distributions are averaged. CA-MoDE achieves an Emotion Recognition Score of 0.3269 on the Body Language Database. By outperforming existing temporal models using only single still images, our framework demonstrates that explicitly modelling structured spatial context can serve as a complementary discriminative proxy for the behavioural dynamics typically captured by video.

</details>


### [258] [Loop-Mamba: A Loop Mamba with Degradation-Aware and Shared Memory for Old Photo Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02346)
*Runci Bai,Yucheng Xin,Pu Wang,Yongcong Wang,Chen Wu,Dianjie Lu,Guijuan Zhang,Pengwen Dai,Guangwei Gao,Siyuan Yao,Zhuoran Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Loop-Mamba是一个轻量级的基于状态空间的旧照片修复框架，通过渐进式状态演化来处理老旧照片中多种耦合退化问题（划痕、裂纹、褪色、模糊、噪声和缺失区域），在SynOld基准上超越了现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老旧照片通常同时遭受多种耦合退化（如划痕、裂纹、褪色、模糊、噪声和缺失区域），严重损害视觉质量和语义内容。传统的迭代式CNN和Transformer修复框架存在计算开销大、梯度稀释等问题，需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: 1) 语义引导退化估计器（SGDE）：联合预测局部退化图和全局退化分数，显式建模异构退化；2) 共享结构记忆Mamba（S²M-Mamba）：跨迭代维护持久恢复状态，实现长程结构重建；3) 利用一阶状态递归传播隐式恢复状态，避免深度特征变换的堆叠；4) 轻量级多方向扫描策略增强方向信息聚合；5) 提出任务导向的旧照片损伤恢复分数（ODRS）评估退化恢复和结构重建保真度。

Result: 在公开的SynOld基准上，Loop-Mamba在传统修复指标和提出的ODRS指标上均持续优于先前的最先进方法。

Conclusion: Loop-Mamba通过将旧照片修复建模为渐进状态演化过程，结合语义引导的退化估计和共享结构记忆的状态传播，在保持轻量级计算的同时实现了优异的修复效果，为旧照片修复领域提供了新的高效范式。
